Result: 人类评估表明DashJudge-8B能有效复现人类判断；消融实验显示交互证据能提升评判器一致性；对前沿模型的实验揭示了持续的渲染、分析和交互失败。

Conclusion: 真实场景下的仪表盘生成仍具挑战性，交互感知的评估能够捕捉静态检查或仅执行检查所遗漏的失败，为仪表盘生成领域提供了更全面可靠的评估基准。

Abstract: Analytic dashboards combine coordinated views and interactions for data exploration and decision-making. Recent models can generate them from data and natural-language goals, but evaluating their usefulness remains difficult. Dashboard generation is open-ended, and neither static appearance nor successful execution alone captures analytical support and interaction quality. We introduce DashArena, to our knowledge the first benchmark for open-ended, task-grounded generation of interactive analytic dashboards. Its key innovation is to require each system to generate both a dashboard and a replayable interaction trajectory. A browser executor replays the trajectory and turns the system's intended analytical workflow into reproducible visual and execution evidence. A VLM judge compares candidates using this evidence, and Bradley--Terry aggregation produces the leaderboard. We further distill the judge into the open-weight DashJudge-8B. Human evaluations show that DashJudge-8B effectively reproduces human judgments and ablations show that interaction evidence improves judge agreement. Experiments with frontier models reveal persistent rendering, analytical, and interaction failures. Together, these results show that realistic dashboard generation remains challenging and that interaction-aware evaluation captures failures missed by static or execution-only checks.

</details>


### [169] [Agentic Instruction Data Selection: Let DataMaster Interpret Your Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10579)
*Fanqi Zhou,Qiaosheng Chen,Zixian Huang,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataMaster，一个指令数据选择代理，通过自然语言理解用户意图并自动编排最优数据选择策略，替代传统的人工配置启发式规则，在数学、医疗和代码领域全面超越静态基线并在多数场景下优于全量数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指令数据选择方法虽引入了多种度量指标，但真实数据集的复杂性使得任何单一指标难以在所有场景中泛化。开发者往往需要为每个新应用手动检查数据并设计启发式规则，这一过程既繁琐又易出错。因此需要一种范式转变，从手动配置转向自动化编排。

Method: 提出指令数据选择代理（DataMaster），它通过解释用户意图并自主组合最优选择策略。用户只需以自然语言描述数据需求，DataMaster即可简化数据筛选流程，消除手动设计策略的负担。

Result: 在数学、医疗和代码领域的大量实验中，DataMaster在大多数设置下优于静态基线，并在相当数量的案例中超过了全量池训练的性能。

Conclusion: DataMaster验证了从手动配置到自动化数据选择编排的范式转变的有效性，并公开发布了实现代码及复现脚本。

Abstract: Although existing instruction data selection methods have introduced various metrics, the inherent complexity of real-world datasets makes it impractical for any single metric to generalize across all scenarios. Developers are thus often forced to manually inspect data and craft heuristic rules for each new application---a tedious and error-prone process. In this paper, we propose a paradigm shift from manual configuration to automated orchestration via the Instruction Data Selection Agent (DataMaster), which interprets user intent and autonomously composes optimal selection strategies. By allowing users to specify data needs through natural language descriptions, DataMaster simplifies data curation and removes the burden of manual strategy design. Extensive experiments across the math, medical, and code domains show that DataMaster outperforms static baselines in most settings and surpasses full-pool training in a substantial number of cases. The implementation of DataMaster and the scripts needed to reproduce the reported pipeline are publicly available at https://github.com/nju-websoft/DataMaster.

</details>


### [170] [HexEval: An Evidence-Driven Hexagonal Framework for Multidimensional Scholar Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10584)
*Xiaokang Qu,Yiting Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HexEval，一个基于证据驱动的六维学者评估框架，将学者评估建模为包含内在质量（研究严谨性、方法创新性、科学贡献）和外部行为（知识转化、研究连贯性、学术影响）两个互补证据层的推理问题，通过异构证据（GitHub、Lens、OpenAlex等）生成可解释、可审计的学者画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学者评估方法主要依赖文献计量指标和声誉代理，基于大语言模型的方法大多聚焦于单篇论文评估而非全面评估学者。因此需要将学者评估形式化为同时考虑内在研究质量和外部可验证学术行为的证据驱动推理问题。

Method: HexEval采用六维评估框架，分为内在层（对匿名代表作品评估研究严谨性、方法创新性、科学贡献）和外部层（利用从GitHub、Lens、OpenAlex等公开可验证来源收集的异构证据，刻画知识转化、研究连贯性、学术影响），并在整个评估过程中保留中间证据、维度特定推理和验证信号，而非产生不透明的聚合分数。

Result: 在六个维度上的实验显示与人类或外部参考标准存在维度相关的协议：结构化校准提高了内在质量的绝对一致性，而外部模块恢复了广泛轨迹和序数影响信号。结果支持基于异构学术证据的证据驱动推理作为可审计AI辅助学者评估的可行范式，同时暴露了公共学术数据在覆盖范围和归因方面的局限性。

Conclusion: 证据驱动的推理优于简单的黑盒评分，能够生成可解释、可审计的学者画像。但公共学术数据的覆盖和归因限制仍是约束因素，提示需要更全面的验证数据源以支持更可靠的学者评估。

Abstract: Scholar assessment plays a fundamental role in faculty recruitment, funding allocation, academic promotion, and talent discovery. Existing scholar assessment methods predominantly rely on bibliometric indicators and reputation proxies, while recent large language model (LLM)-based approaches mainly focus on evaluating individual research papers rather than comprehensively assessing scholars. We argue that scholar assessment should be formulated as an evidence-driven reasoning problem that jointly considers intrinsic research quality and externally verifiable scholarly behavior. To this end, we propose HexEval, an evidence-driven hexagonal framework for multidimensional scholar assessment. HexEval explicitly organizes scholar assessment into two complementary evidence layers. The intrinsic layer evaluates anonymized representative works along three dimensions, namely research rigor, methodological innovation, and scientific contribution, whereas the external layer characterizes scholars through knowledge translation, research coherence, and academic impact using heterogeneous evidence collected from GitHub, Lens, OpenAlex, and other publicly verifiable sources. Instead of producing opaque aggregate scores, HexEval preserves intermediate evidence, dimension-specific rationales, and verification signals throughout the evaluation process, enabling interpretable and auditable scholar profiles. Experiments across all six dimensions show dimension-dependent agreement with human or external reference criteria: structured calibration improves absolute agreement for intrinsic quality, while the external modules recover broad trajectory and ordinal impact signals. These results support evidence-driven reasoning over heterogeneous scholarly evidence as a promising paradigm for auditable AI-assisted scholar assessment, while exposing the coverage and attribution limitations of public scholarly data.

</details>


### [171] [Curate Before You Connect: Identity and Ontology Tagging in a Production Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10644)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文描述了一个政府文档知识图谱的构建系统，包含记录身份阶梯（record-identity ladder）决定实体合并策略、多类本体标注层及人工策展队列。图谱含537,157个实体和2,198,567条关系，源自98,795份政府文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体解析（entity resolution）中的身份决定是破坏性的：不同于类型标注错误可在事后修正，两个记录一旦合并就无法分离且不留错误痕迹。作者主张自动化线应划在"仅标记候选合并"而非"自动执行合并"上，以避免过度合并导致不可逆的数据损坏。

Method: 1) 记录身份阶梯：依次依据标识符列、名称列、显示名及类型限定位置（type-scoped position）而非名称相似度来决定"同一性"；表内去重使用该阶梯，图谱写入则使用较粗的规范名键（canonical-name key），精确相等即自动合并。2) 多类本体标注：采用锚定证据（anchored evidence）机制，要求实体名称与类索引的匹配必须落在锚定上下文中，防止"实例标签被误当作类型断言"而虚构分类。3) 策展队列：累计48,403个待处理提案对775个人工决策，用于量化一致性债（conformance debt）和处理次分类问题。

Result: 锚定证据机制将富样本上的角色分配从36例降至4例，且全部经人工确认正确；图谱的一致性债被量化；次分类用于补偿错误的父类（mis-parented primary class）；策展队列增长至48,403个待处理候选，对应775个人工决策。作者明确报告未提供身份基准（identity benchmark），且规范的过度合并问题在构造上不可检测。

Conclusion: 作者论证（而非演示）实体解析的自动化边界应位于"仅产生合并候选供人工审核"，理由是合并操作是不可逆的破坏性操作；安全策略采用保守自动化——系统只标记候选而不自动合并。该策略源于一次事故：两个同名变体被合并，损坏了正确记录并从无关文档中删除了8个实体。

Abstract: Extraction produces candidate entities and relationships; writing them into a graph is where identity is decided, and identity decisions are destructive in a way extraction errors are not. A wrong type can be corrected later, but two records merged under one identity cannot be separated once their properties have been combined, and the merge leaves no error behind. This paper describes the ingestion and ontology-tagging layer that turns a validated extraction stream into a knowledge graph of 537,157 entities and 2,198,567 relationships drawn from 98,795 government documents. We describe a record-identity ladder that decides sameness from identifier columns, name columns, display names and type-scoped position rather than from name similarity. The ladder governs de-duplication within parsed tables, while the graph write applies a coarser canonical-name key, so records sharing a canonical name merge automatically on exact equality. We argue rather than demonstrate that this is where the automation line belongs: no identity benchmark is reported, and the over-merges the key permits are undetectable by construction. That policy, under which entity resolution only ever flags candidates, followed an incident in which two surface forms of one name were merged, corrupting a correct record and deleting eight entities from an unrelated document. We then describe multi-class ontology tagging and an evidence asymmetry we did not anticipate: an entity name is an instance label rather than a type assertion, so matching name fragments against a class index invents classifications. Requiring anchored evidence cut role assignments on an enriched sample from 36 to 4, all confirmed correct. We quantify the graph's conformance debt, show secondary classifications compensating for a mis-parented primary class, and describe a curation queue grown to 48,403 pending proposals against 775 human decisions.

</details>


### [172] [Decision-Aware Approximation of Belief Functions for Evidential Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10650)
*Sohaib Afifi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种决策感知的mass函数近似方法，与传统基于Jaccard或Jousselme距离的表示感知近似不同，该方法针对由证据成本驱动的线性组合优化问题，通过最小化决策遗憾（regret）而非保持两个mass函数的接近度来压缩焦元。作者证明了在真实最优处的一个一点界将遗憾局部化，将其转化为标量情形的精确动态规划，并扩展为在线版本。实验表明决策感知压缩器比表示感知压缩更少翻转决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，降低mass函数焦元数量的方法基于内在距离（如Jaccard或Jousselme距离），保持近似与原mass函数作为证据体的接近度。但当mass函数用于带证据成本的线性组合优化问题时，真正需要保留的不是mass函数本身的接近度，而是由此引发的决策质量。现有方法可能导致决策翻转，因此需要一种以决策为导向的近似方法。

Method: 提出决策感知近似方法，针对决策的遗憾进行优化：用更廉价的近似进行决策，但在真实mass函数下评估。具体包括：(1) 证明一个一点界，将遗憾局部化于真实最优处；(2) 将遗憾界转化为标量情形的精确动态规划；(3) 扩展为在线版本，在最终成本未知前剪枝焦元；在最小最短路径上进行验证。

Result: 在最小最短路径上，距离最优近似会翻转决策，而决策感知合并保留了决策；这在不可忽略比例的随机实例上发生。实验中，决策感知压缩器在翻转决策的频率上低于表示感知压缩，且同时在线性准则和非线性代理读出方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 当mass函数用于下游组合优化决策时，决策质量的保持比表示距离的接近更为重要。决策感知近似方法通过局部化遗憾并利用动态规划优化，能够比传统表示感知方法更可靠地保持决策不变，为证据理论在决策场景下的近似压缩提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reducing the number of focal elements of a mass function is classically driven by an intrinsic distance, such as Jaccard or Jousselme, that keeps the approximation close to the original as a body of evidence. We consider instead the case where the mass function feeds a linear combinatorial optimisation problem with evidential costs. What should then be preserved is not the closeness of the two mass functions, but the quality of the decision they induce. We introduce a decision-aware approximation that targets the regret of the decision: one decides with the cheaper approximation and is evaluated under the true mass function. On a minimal shortest path, the distance-optimal approximation flips the decision while a decision-aware merge preserves it, and this occurs on a non-negligible fraction of random instances. We prove a one-point bound that localises the regret at the true optimum, turn it into an exact dynamic program for the scalar case, and extend it to an online version that prunes focal elements before the final cost is known. In experiments the decision-aware compressor flips the decision less often than representation-aware compression, for both the linear criterion and a non-linear proxy read-out.

</details>


### [173] [Operationalising Relative Causal Knowledge: Backbone Identifiability from Private Reports on a Shared Outcome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10664)
*Fabrizio Russo,Mark Somers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究因果关系知识相对性（RCK）框架下的先验可识别性问题：当多个智能体各自拥有不同的结构因果模型但共享一个干预一致的抽象（backbone）时，该backbone是否能由各智能体的局部因果知识唯一确定。结果表明，在基本的双智能体共同效应（common-effect）情形下，局部因果边缘信息无法唯一识别backbone，存在无穷多个联合干预核产生相同的局部报告；但在加性可分离、且智能体交流因果识别的响应函数时，可识别性得以恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCK框架假设不同结构因果模型的智能体通过共享的干预一致抽象（backbone）交换因果知识，但这一传输机制预设了一个尚未回答的问题：backbone何时能由智能体各自的私有因果知识确定？若不可确定，则因果知识的跨智能体传输在原则上就是不确定的。

Method: 论文分析基本的两智能体共同效应情形：两个私有原因共同影响一个共享结果，每个智能体仅识别与自己视角相关的单原因因果边缘。作者在标准兼容性、非退化性和局部重叠假设下，证明局部因果边缘无法唯一识别backbone（非可识别性定理），然后给出条件性恢复结果：加性可分离性消除隐藏交互自由度，但观测残差汇总仍不足；当智能体交流因果识别的响应函数时可实现可识别。最后用教育增值（value-added）例子说明为何这首先是通信问题，其次才是政策组合问题。

Result: （1）在基本两智能体共同效应情形下，局部因果边缘不能唯一确定联合干预核——存在无穷多个联合干预核产生完全相同的私有报告，但在联合干预上不一致；（2）加性可分离性虽消除了隐藏交互自由度，但仅靠观测残差汇总仍不足以实现识别；（3）当智能体进一步交流因果识别的响应函数时，可识别性可以实现；（4）教育增值案例表明该问题本质上是通信问题，而非政策组合问题。

Conclusion: 因果知识在异质智能体网络中的传输，其前提（backbone的存在性和唯一性）并非自动成立。在常见设定下，仅交换局部因果边缘信息不足以唯一确定共享的backbone。要实现可识别，需要更强条件（如加性可分离性）以及智能体间更丰富的通信内容（因果识别的响应函数），说明这本质上是通信协议设计问题。

Abstract: The Relativity of Causal Knowledge (RCK) explains how a network of agents with different structural causal models can exchange causal knowledge through a shared interventionally consistent abstraction, or backbone. We ask the prior identification question that this transport mechanism presupposes: when is that backbone determined by the agents' private causal knowledge? In the basic two-agent common-effect case, two private causes influence one shared outcome and each agent identifies only the single-cause causal marginal relevant to its own perspective. We show that, under standard compatibility, non-degeneracy, and local overlap assumptions, those local causal marginals do not identify a unique backbone. Infinitely many joint intervention kernels can induce exactly the same private reports while disagreeing on joint interventions. We then give a conditional recovery result. Additive separability removes the hidden interaction degree of freedom, but observational residual summaries remain insufficient. Identification becomes possible when agents communicate causally identified response functions. An education value-added example illustrates why this is first a communication problem, and only then a policy-composition problem.

</details>


### [174] [VERDICT: Training-Free Step-Wise Verification of Multimodal Reasoning via Disagreement-Aware Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10665)
*Rohit Sinha,Kunal Tilaganji,Tanuja Ganu,Nagarajan Natarajan,Amit Sharma,Vineeth Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VERDICT，一种无需训练的、领域无关的逐步验证方法，通过显式利用多模态大语言模型验证器之间的不一致性作为验证信号，将验证问题形式化为耦合评分问题（协调博弈），并通过闭式解计算共识分数，实现不一致感知的过滤和稳定性感知的排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型生成的推理链常包含细微错误导致错误答案。现有验证方法存在明显局限：要么需要昂贵的标注监督且跨任务表现不一致，要么通过简单聚合多个来源的分数，忽略了关键洞察——当验证器分数不一致时，这种分歧本身携带关于推理步骤是否真正有效的重要信息。

Method: 将验证问题形式化为耦合评分问题，可解释为协调博弈，具有唯一的闭式均衡解：一致信号表示有效步骤，分歧揭示不稳定性。基于此提出VERDICT（VERification via Disagreement-Informed Coupled Thresholding），通过闭式解计算共识分数，支持不一致感知过滤和稳定性感知的推理步骤排序。该方法无需训练且领域无关。

Result: 在六个基准上评估，VERDICT相比基础模型最多提升+5.95%，与需要大量监督的领域特定评判器表现相当，证明跨模态一致性能提供稳健的验证信号，无需任务特定适配。

Conclusion: VERDICT是首个使跨模态不一致结构显式化且可操作的免训练验证器，其成功表明跨模态的一致/分歧信号本身即可作为可靠的验证依据，为多模态推理链验证提供了无需监督的新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Existing approaches either require expensive labelled supervision with inconsistent cross-task performance or aggregate scores from multiple sources by simple aggregations, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasoning step is truly valid or not. We formalise this as a coupled scoring problem among disparate, frozen verifiers, interpretable as a coordination game with a unique closed-form equilibrium where agreement signals valid steps while disagreement reveals instability. Towards this end, we propose a training-free domain-agnostic step-wise verification approach we call VERDICT: VERification via Disagreement-Informed Coupled Thresholding. To our knowledge, VERDICT is the first training-free verifier that makes the structure of cross-modal disagreement explicit and actionable. It computes consensus scores through a closed-form solution, enabling both disagreement-aware filtering and stability-conscious ranking of reasoning steps. Evaluated across six benchmarks, \method consistently improves over the base model by up to +5.95%, and performs competitively with domain-specific critics that demand extensive supervision, demonstrating that cross-modal agreement provides robust verification signals without task-specific adaptation and Training-Free Verification

</details>


### [175] [FITTER: Vocabulary-Agnostic Cross-Domain Inference on Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10668)
*Jiaxin Pan,Mojtaba Nayyeri,Osama Mohammed,Daniel Hernandez,Rongchuan Zhang,Cheng Cheng,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FITTER是首个完全归纳式（fully-inductive）的时序知识图谱链接预测模型，支持跨域迁移：推理图中可包含训练时完全未见过的实体、关系名和时间戳。通过相对时间顺序编码和消息传递机制，FITTER实现了与词汇无关（vocabulary-agnostic）的结构化学习，在六个跨域基准上一致优于归纳式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序知识图谱补全方法假设推理所需的实体、关系名和时间戳在训练时已知，这限制每个模型只能应用于单一图谱和词汇表。然而语义网中的时序知识图谱异构性强、跨域广泛，需要能够支持跨领域迁移的模型。

Method: FITTER采用三个关键设计：(1) 每个谓词通过其与其他谓词的交互模式以及时间的编码来表示，而非依赖绝对名称；(2) 使用相对顺序而非绝对顺序的时间编码，保证时序表示具有时间平移不变性（time-shift invariant）；(3) 通过消息传递机制融合局部与全局时序上下文，生成与词汇无关的实体嵌入。

Result: FITTER在六个不同领域、时间粒度和跨度各异的时序知识图谱基准上进行了跨域、跨图迁移评估，无需重新训练即可一致性地优于归纳式基线。

Conclusion: 实验表明，词汇无关的结构化学习是语义网异构知识图谱推理的可行基础，FITTER为跨域时序知识图谱链接预测提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs are central to many uses of the Semantic Web, but existing completion methods assume the entities, relation names, and timestamps to be reasoned about are already known at training time, restricting each model to a single graph and vocabulary. We propose FITTER, the first fully-inductive structural model for temporal knowledge graph link prediction that supports cross-domain transfer: the inference graph may contain entirely unseen entities, relation names, and timestamps drawn from a different domain. FITTER represents each predicate by its interaction patterns with others and time through encodings of relative rather than absolute ordering; message-passing fuses local and global temporal context to produce vocabulary-agnostic embeddings. We prove the temporal encoding is time-shift invariant and evaluate FITTER on cross-domain, cross-graph transfer over six temporal knowledge graph benchmarks of diverse domains, granularities, and time spans. FITTER consistently outperforms inductive baselines without retraining, indicating that vocabulary-agnostic structural learning is a viable foundation for inference over the heterogeneous knowledge graphs of the Semantic Web.

</details>


### [176] [REDAgentBench: Executable Red Teaming and Faithful Measurement of LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10669)
*Zixing Chen,Xingyuan Liu,Jie Zhu,Huaixia Dou,Shuo Jiang,Junhui Li,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REDAgentBench，一个可执行的LLM智能体红队测试与安全评测框架。该框架从显式安全约束和智能体系统漏洞中派生攻击，在隔离服务沙盒中运行，并通过服务收据和最终状态变化验证有害影响。基准包含1,661个案例、5个服务面，在6个模型和3个智能体框架上宏观平均攻击成功率为65.69%。研究发现ASR随框架和证据视图变化，并揭示"识别-执行鸿沟"（Recognition-Execution Gap）：近五分之一的已确认违规发生在智能体已陈述相关约束或风险之后。训练无关的策略提醒在匹配回放中将已确认违规降低超过70个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体安全评估往往将安全简化归结为单一攻击成功率（ASR），混淆了暴露、执行、观察和判定等不同环节，可能将实际违规与证据可见性混为一谈。需要一个可执行的框架来准确区分这些因素并实现忠实的安全评测。

Method: REDAgentBench包含三个核心组件：（1）从显式安全约束和智能体系统漏洞中系统化派生攻击；（2）在隔离服务沙盒中执行攻击场景；（3）基于服务收据和最终状态变化验证有害效果。基准覆盖1,661个案例和5类服务面。此外引入状态基础诊断队列（state-grounded diagnostic cohort）来定位违规发生时智能体是否已陈述相关约束，以及训练无关的"策略提醒"作为干预手段。

Result: 在6个模型和3个智能体框架上，宏观平均ASR为65.69%；报告的ASR随智能体框架和证据视图而变化，评估上下文披露也会改变执行行为。在状态基础诊断队列中发现，近五分之一的已确认违规（具有解析的动作锚点）发生在智能体已陈述相关约束或风险之后，揭示识别-执行鸿沟。训练无关的策略提醒在匹配回放中使已确认违规减少超过70个百分点。

Conclusion: 可执行的评测框架能够改善安全测量的忠实度，并识别出可干预的改进点（如策略提醒）。识别-执行鸿沟表明，许多违规并非源于对约束的无知，而是意识与执行之间的脱节，这为安全干预提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents combine language-based reasoning with external tools to perform complex tasks. Adversarial inputs can exploit interactions between the agent and its environment, causing the agent to violate safety policies during execution. Yet existing evaluations often reduce agent safety to a single attack success rate (ASR), collapsing exposure, execution, observation, and adjudication and potentially conflating actual violations with evidence visibility. We introduce REDAgentBench, an executable framework for autonomous red-teaming and faithful measurement. It derives attacks from explicit safety constraints and associated agent-system vulnerabilities, runs them in isolated service sandboxes, and verifies harmful effects from service receipts and final-state changes. The benchmark contains 1,661 cases across five service surfaces. Across six models and three agent harnesses, macro-average ASR is 65.69%; reported ASR varies with harness and evidence view, while evaluation-context disclosure changes execution behavior. In a state-grounded diagnostic cohort, almost one in five confirmed violations with resolved action anchors occurs after the agent states the relevant constraint or risk, revealing a Recognition--Execution Gap. Finally, a training-free policy reminder reduces confirmed violations by more than 70 percentage points in matched replay. These findings show that executable evaluation can improve safety measurement and identify actionable intervention points.

</details>


### [177] [Self-Correcting Long-Horizon Search Agents via Tree-Structured Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10676)
*Aijun Yang,Qianxue Guo,Ziyi Huang,Yuxuan Chen,Shiyou Qian,Jian Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReTree，一种用于LLM搜索Agent的自纠错树状结构记忆机制，通过有界上下文构建和证据溯源，在保持搜索效率的同时提升回答准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的搜索Agent通过多步与环境交互来回答问题，但将完整执行轨迹提供给LLM会导致上下文无界增长并引入噪声。现有压缩方法在减少上下文时会丢失重要细节，且当检索到错误事实时往往只替换事实而不修复由其衍生的下游推理。

Method: ReTree将搜索过程建模为证据树，节点存储有界摘要、证据和修订历史。当新检索到的证据与先前声明冲突时，ReTree回溯到引入该声明的节点，替换过时证据，重新生成摘要，剪除受影响的分支并恢复搜索。通过证据溯源确保冲突定位可靠，最终声明可追溯至检索段落。

Result: 在四个公开问答和搜索基准上，ReTree一致优于Full-Trajectory ReAct，回答准确率最多提升25.6个百分点；Full-Trajectory ReAct的平均每步最大推理上下文是ReTree的1.27-1.51倍。

Conclusion: ReTree作为一种有效的自纠错记忆抽象机制，能够支持长时程搜索任务，在保持上下文有界的同时实现比完整轨迹方法更优的准确率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based search agents answer questions through multi-step interactions with external environments. However, providing complete execution trajectories to the LLM causes unbounded context growth and introduces noise. Existing compression methods reduce context at the cost of important details and often replace erroneous facts without repairing downstream reasoning derived from them. To address this problem, we propose ReTree, a self-correcting tree-structured memory mechanism for search agents. ReTree constructs a bounded per-step reasoning context while preserving source-linked evidence. It models search as an evidence tree whose nodes store bounded summaries, evidence, and revision histories. When newly retrieved evidence contradicts an earlier claim, ReTree traces back to the node where the claim was introduced, replaces outdated evidence, regenerates summaries, prunes affected branches, and resumes search. Source-grounded evidence provenance supports reliable conflict localization and keeps final claims traceable to retrieved passages. Experiments on four public question-answering and search benchmarks show that ReTree consistently outperforms Full-Trajectory ReAct, improving answer accuracy by up to 25.6 percentage points (pp); the average maximum per-step reasoning context of Full-Trajectory ReAct is $1.27$--$1.51\times$ that of ReTree. These results establish ReTree as an effective self-correcting memory abstraction for long-horizon search.

</details>


### [178] [Ex-Omni-2D: Expressive Omni-Modal Dialogue Models with Native Visual Presence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10720)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zhipeng Li,Xiaoying Tang,Tianshu Yu,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ex-Omni-2D，一个全模态对话框架，能同时生成文本、个性化语音和参考条件驱动的视频回应。通过视觉思维计划（VTP）和共享声学-时间接口，模型从异构数据中学习，并使用教师-学生蒸馏策略实现流式视频生成，端到端RTF为1.293。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态对话模型虽然能理解多模态输入并合成语音回复，但回复缺乏视觉具身性（visually disembodied），即没有能够同步生成与语音和文本协调的、参考条件驱动的视频面部动画或虚拟形象，限制了对话体验的完整性和自然度。

Method: 提出Ex-Omni-2D框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）Visual Thought Plan（VTP）——预测场景、情感和动作的结构化计划；（2）共享声学-时间接口——预测原生多码本语音单元，这些单元同时解码为语音并与视频帧在线对齐，使回复路径和虚拟形象路径可从异构数据（语音、对话、虚拟形象视频）中学习；（3）教师-学生蒸馏——全序列视频生成器作为教师，蒸馏为少步块因果流式学生模型，其Prefix Streaming机制在连续块中保持清洁的潜在表示以减少累积延迟退化。

Result: 在四步推理下，完整的四GPU流水线在400×720/720×400分辨率下实现端到端RTF（实时因子）为1.293，提供一个实际可用的质量-效率平衡工作点。论文表明框架避免了大规模查询-文本-语音-视频监督数据的需求。

Conclusion: Ex-Omni-2D通过视觉思维计划和共享声学-时间接口，实现了文本、个性化语音和参考条件驱动的视频协调生成，使对话模型从"仅有声的单体回复"进化为"具有视觉具身性的协调响应"，并在实时性上达到了实用的水平。

Abstract: Omni-modal dialogue models can understand multimodal inputs and synthesize spoken replies, yet their responses remain visually disembodied. We introduce \textbf{Ex-Omni-2D}, an omni-modal dialogue framework that generates a coordinated response comprising text, personalized speech, and reference-conditioned video. Given a multimodal query, reference image, and reference audio, the model predicts a structured \textit{Visual Thought Plan} (VTP) describing scene, emotion, and motion, followed by response text and native multi-codebook speech units. These units form a shared acoustic-temporal interface: they are decoded into speech and aligned online with video frames. This interface enables the response and avatar pathways to be learned from heterogeneous speech, dialogue, and avatar-video data, avoiding the need for large-scale query--text--speech--video supervision. A full-sequence Video Generator serves as the primary Teacher. For efficient incremental generation, we further distill it into a few-step block-causal \emph{Streaming Student} whose Prefix Streaming mechanism carries a clean latent across consecutive chunks to reduce cumulative late-chunk degradation. With four-step inference, the complete four-GPU pipeline achieves an end-to-end RTF of 1.293 at $400\times720$/$720\times400$, providing a practical quality--efficiency operating point.

</details>


### [179] [Tree-of-Ideas: Automated Research Ideation via Cross-Trajectory Reasoning over Scholarly Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10740)
*Xun Li,Yiying Yang,Pengtao Li,Xiao Yao,Suyu Liu,Xiaoyang Ye,Ziyu Lu,Yuan Yao,Yangning Li,Yinghui Li,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Tree-of-Ideas (ToI)两阶段框架，通过EvoTrace从引用关系重建分支化学术轨迹，再通过EvoAgent跨轨迹推理生成有根据的研究想法。在六个AI研究主题上达到6.27分，接近人类论文参考分数(6.29)，超越最强基线(5.36)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究构思方法要么将论文当作非结构化上下文处理，要么将学术演化建模为孤立的引用链，忽视了研究轨迹之间的交互作用。需要一种能追踪研究问题与解决方案跨文献演化过程并捕捉轨迹间交互的方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) EvoTrace从引用关系重建分支化学术轨迹，追踪演化中的方法、已解决问题和未解决缺口；(2) EvoAgent跨轨迹推理，识别收敛问题和互补解决方案，生成有根据的研究想法。

Result: 在六个AI研究主题上，ToI在自动方法中得分最高(6.27 vs 最强基线5.36，10分制)，Novelty得分6.36，Groundedness得分7.00，总分接近人类论文参考分数6.29。

Conclusion: 跨路径进化推理能显著提升研究构思的质量，ToI验证了将学术演化建模为分支轨迹并跨轨迹推理的价值，其生成的研究想法在综合质量和落地性上均接近人类专家水准。

Abstract: Effective research ideation requires moving beyond a static understanding of prior work to trace how research problems and solutions evolve across the literature. Existing methods either treat papers as unstructured context or model scholarly evolution as isolated citation chains, overlooking interactions among research trajectories. We propose Tree-of-Ideas (ToI), a two-stage framework. EvoTrace reconstructs branching scholarly trajectories from citations, tracking evolving methods, resolved problems, and gaps. EvoAgent then reasons across trajectories to identify convergent problems and complementary solutions, generating grounded research ideas. Across six AI research topics, ToI achieves the highest score among automatic methods (6.27 vs. 5.36 for the strongest baseline on a 10-point scale), with strong Novelty (6.36) and Groundedness (7.00). Also, its score approaches that of human-paper references (6.29), demonstrating the value of cross-path evolutionary reasoning.

</details>


### [180] [Compositional Benchmark Synthesis for Hierarchical Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10765)
*Farnaz Soleimani,Abdelghani Chibani,Yacine Amirat,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基准生成与评估框架，从单一标签的动作语料库合成四层层次化意图基准（动作、活动、低级意图LLI、高级意图HLI），通过过渡模型和覆盖感知采样器生成15,002个片段，并通过设计避免循环监督风险，验证了各基线模型存在0.13-0.17宏F1的组合性保留差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类行为识别需要跨抽象层次（从原子动作到长期意图）的语义层次标注数据，但现有大型语料库仅提供孤立的原子级标签缺乏时间组合，而复合活动语料库则层次浅、领域窄、层次固定。需要一种方法从丰富的扁平标签动作语料库合成层次化意图基准。

Method: 提出一个基准生成与评估框架：从扁平单标签动作语料库合成四层层次化意图基准，保留动作层真实的预提取特征；通过过渡模型在主体一致性约束下组装片段；使用覆盖感知采样器将主体使用Gini系数从0.566降至0.248；将片段生成规则与评估用的一阶逻辑规则保持不相交以避免循环监督；并采用逻辑无关基线和顺序破坏控制进行生成器一致性检验。

Result: 合成了15,002个片段。四个参考基线的组合性保留差距为0.13-0.17宏F1，包括识别最优的图感知模型也未能缩小该差距，表明这是基准的结构性属性而非模型伪影。逻辑无关基线在固有数据比率之上违反了保留语义规则，顺序破坏控制导致宏F1在种子变异范围内变化。

Conclusion: 组合性保留差距在所有基线中一致出现，证实了基准的结构性难度；逻辑无关基线与顺序破坏控制验证了生成器的有效性和基准的可信度。本体、过渡模型和生成器均已开源，支持基准的再生与扩展。

Abstract: Recognizing human behavior across levels of abstraction, from atomic actions to long-horizon intentions, requires data annotated along a semantic hierarchy. Large corpora provide isolated, atomically labeled clips without temporal composition, whereas recorded composite-activity corpora offer shallow, domain-narrow, fixedhierarchies. A benchmark-generation and evaluation frameworkis proposed that synthesizes a four-level hierarchical-intention benchmark, spanning actions, activities, low-level intentions (LLIs), and high-level intentions (HLIs), from a flat single-label action corpus while retaining real pre-extracted features at the action level. Episodes are assembled by a transition model under a subject-consistency constraint, and a coverage-aware sampler reduces the subject usage Gini from 0.566 to 0.248. Synthesizing such a benchmark raises a circular-supervision risk that recorded datasets avoid: if the rules generating the episodes also govern the evaluation, models can succeed by recovering the generator rather than through genuine reasoning. Validity is addressed by design, holding sequence-generation rules disjoint from the first-order-logic rules used at evaluation. The instantiation yields 15,002 episodes. Four reference baselines from different model families characterize difficulty, not as recognition methods. A compositional held-out gap of 0.13 to 0.17 macro-F1 appears across all baselines, including a graph-aware model that recognizes best yet does not close the gap, indicating a structural property of the benchmark rather than a model artifact. A logic-free baseline still violates the held-out semantic rules above their intrinsic data rate, and the order-destroying control changes macro-F1 within seed variation, serving as a generator-consistency check. Theontology, transition model, and generator are released so the benchmark can beregenerated and extended.

</details>


### [181] [SkillLens: Visual Skill Cards for Retrieval-Augmented GUI Action Prediction and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10775)
*Zhou Liu,Ligang Huang,Zeli Su,Zewei Pan,Zhaoyang Han,Xing Chen,Yuanfeng Song,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉技能卡片（VSCs），一种状态条件化的记忆表示，用于将可复用的GUI操作流程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定，解决计算机代理缺乏视觉程序性记忆的问题。相应的SkillLens框架从交互轨迹构建VSC并在推理时检索扩展，CardDistill利用VSC证据作为特权教师上下文训练无需检索的学生模型。在Multimodal-Mind2Web和WebLINX-BrowserGym上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理虽然能感知丰富的软件界面，但其决策往往缺乏视觉程序性记忆：它们可能识别出单个控件，却不知道当前处于哪个熟悉的工作流程、下一步哪个控件重要、或什么证据能确认进展。原始交互轨迹虽保留此类信息但冗长且嘈杂，文本技能又忽略了使程序可应用的关键视觉状态。

Method: 提出视觉技能卡片（VSCs）作为状态条件化记忆表示，绑定可复用程序与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号。SkillLens通过Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card从异构交互经验构建VSC，推理时检索相关卡片并选择性扩展证据，交由固定的视觉-语言模型执行器进行有依据的GUI动作预测。CardDistill则利用VSC证据作为特权教师上下文，训练一个在运行时无需卡片检索的学生模型。

Result: 在Multimodal-Mind2Web上，SkillLens将冻结的GPT-5.4-mini执行器Step SR提升11.6个点；在WebLINX-BrowserGym上Overall提升2.9个点。CardDistill将学生模型Qwen3-VL-2B的对应指标分别提升12.0和3.2个点。

Conclusion: 视觉技能卡片作为一种可验证、可检索的视觉程序性记忆表示，能有效增强GUI代理的行为决策能力；既可通过推理时检索增强冻结的大模型执行器，也可通过蒸馏让学生模型摆脱运行时检索依赖，验证了该表示的可迁移性和通用性。

Abstract: Computer-using agents can perceive rich software interfaces, yet their decisions often lack visual procedural memory: they may recognize individual controls without identifying which familiar workflow is active, which control matters next, or what evidence would confirm progress. Raw interaction traces preserve such information but are long and noisy to condition on, whereas text-only skills often omit the visual state that makes a procedure applicable. We introduce Visual Skill Cards (VSCs), a state-conditioned memory representation that binds reusable procedures with applicability cues, visual evidence, and verification signals. SkillLens constructs VSCs from heterogeneous interaction experience through Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card and, at inference time, retrieves relevant cards and selectively expands only the evidence needed by a fixed visual-language model executor for grounded GUI action prediction. The same representation also supports CardDistill, which uses VSC evidence as privileged teacher context to train a student that acts without runtime card retrieval. Across Multimodal-Mind2Web and WebLINX-BrowserGym, SkillLens improves the frozen GPT-5.4-mini executor by +11.6 points in Step SR and +2.9 points in Overall, respectively; CardDistill further improves the corresponding student-only Qwen3-VL-2B metrics by +12.0 and +3.2 points.

</details>


### [182] [ChemWorld: Programmable Chemical Worlds for Controlled and Replayable Agent Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10792)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChemWorld，一个可编程化学环境系统，将可复用的过程和观察组件编译为可执行世界，实现受控、可重放且可审计的实验环境，作为物理实验室的补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主化学研究日益依赖智能体可反复行动、观察和适应的环境。物理实验室提供真实材料证据但成本高且难以进行严格匹配的干预对照，而大多数数字环境又保持底层实验世界固定不变，缺乏灵活性和可控性。

Method: ChemWorld将公开实验契约与评估者拥有的化学和材料定律分离，支持研究者灵活变更世界组成和操作条件，或修改单一隐藏定律而保持公开任务和交互条件不变。通过事务性执行记录操作、失败、资源变化和状态转换，实现完整环境-行动轨迹的精确重放和审计。系统进行了全量普查资格测试、八个确定性实验案例和六个父-子世界分叉配对实验来验证功能。

Result: 全量普查资格覆盖了参考注册表、52个生成组成以及模块、接口、编译和无效操作测试。八个确定性实验案例展示了共享生命周期语义、故障恢复和精确重放能力；六个父-子世界分叉对在匹配的公开条件下隔离了单一私有定律干预的效果。独立智能体也通过相同公开接口在非参考世界完成了完整生命周期。

Conclusion: 在声明的组件和模型范围内，ChemWorld为系统化多样化学世界中的实验研究提供了受控且可重放的基底，可与物理实验室证据和校准互补使用。

Abstract: Autonomous chemistry increasingly depends on environments in which agents can repeatedly act, observe, and adapt.Physical laboratories provide essential real-material evidence but are costly to repeat and difficult to use for tightly matched interventions, whereas most digital environments keep the underlying experimental world largely fixed. We introduce ChemWorld, a programmable chemical environment in which reusable process and observation components are compiled into executable worlds. ChemWorld separates the public experimental contract available to an agent from evaluator-owned chemical and material laws. Researchers can therefore vary world composition and operating conditions, or change a single hidden law while holding the public task and interaction conditions fixed. Transactional execution records operations, failures, resource changes, and state transitions, allowing complete environment-action trajectories to be replayed exactly and audited. Full-census qualification covered the reference registry, 52 generated compositions, and module, interface, compilation, and invalid-action tests. Eight deterministic experimental cases demonstrated shared lifecycle semantics, failure recovery, and exact replay, while six parent-child world-fork pairs isolated the effects of single private-law interventions under matched public conditions. An independent agent also completed a full lifecycle in a non-reference world through the same public interface. Within the declared component and model domain, ChemWorld provides a controlled and replayable substrate for studying experimentation across systematically varied chemical worlds, complementary to physical-laboratory evidence and calibration.

</details>


### [183] [EvoMem: Memory-Augmented Evolution for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10795)
*Viktor Volkov,Valentin Khrulkov,Andrey V. Galichin,Danil Sivtsov,Nikita Glazkov,Olga Volkova,Konstantin Pchelin,Iaroslav Bespalov,Dmitry V. Dylov,Petr Anokhin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoMem是一种为基于LLM的演化程序搜索设计的持久记忆架构，通过将成功的突变事件转化为结构化的任务感知建议并跨运行复用，以捕获和重用可迁移的突变知识。实验在几何优化、多跳问答、GPU内核优化等基准上显示大多数设置的平均指标或搜索速度均有提升，但存在任务间变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的演化框架在每次运行后丢弃成功突变中蕴含的可复用知识，导致反复发现相似思路，限制了跨运行和跨任务的迁移学习机会。

Method: EvoMem采用两阶段架构：(1) 事后提取阶段——每次演化运行结束后，将成功的突变事件提取并存储为带有来源追踪（provenance）的结构化任务感知建议；(2) 运行中检索阶段——在后续演化过程中，根据当前任务和程序上下文检索一小部分相关指令来指导突变。

Result: 在几何优化、多跳问答、GPU内核优化及相关基准上的实验中，大多数评估设置下目标指标或搜索速度均获得正向平均改进，但结果也揭示了不同任务之间的性能变异性。

Conclusion: 持久记忆能够减少部分冗余探索，改善LLM驱动演化搜索中成功策略的复用与适配，为跨运行和跨任务学习提供了有效证据。

Abstract: Successful mutation strategies in evolutionary code search may contain reusable knowledge that is useful beyond a single run, and in some cases may transfer across related tasks and domains. However, existing LLM-driven evolutionary frameworks largely discard such knowledge, repeatedly rediscovering similar ideas and limiting opportunities for cross-run and cross-task learning. We introduce EvoMem, a persistent memory architecture for LLM-based evolutionary program search that captures and reuses candidate mutation knowledge. EvoMem converts successful mutation events into structured, task-aware advice for future runs. It operates in two phases: after each run, it extracts and stores promising ideas with provenance, and during subsequent evolution, it retrieves a small set of relevant instructions based on the current task and program context to guide mutation. Across geometric optimization, multi-hop question answering, GPU kernel optimization, and related benchmarks, our experiments show positive average improvements in target metrics or search speed for most evaluated settings, while also revealing variability across tasks. Overall, EvoMem provides evidence that persistent memory can reduce some redundant exploration and improve the reuse and adaptation of successful strategies in LLM-driven evolutionary search.

</details>


### [184] [Hypothesis Frontier: Verifier Guided LLM and Symbolic Search for First-Order Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10843)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hypothesis Frontier，一种验证器引导的神经符号框架，用于一阶概念合成任务。该框架在每一轮中评估LLM生成的每个公式在所有训练对象上的表现，保留最强验证假设，并利用剩余错误引导后续生成；符号处理负责修复无效公式并简化可行的公式。实验表明，在相同模型、问题集和LLM轮次预算下，该方法比重复原始提示生成解决显著更多的问题，且精确简化在保持训练预测不变的前提下缩短了许多公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶概念合成需要系统推断单一公式以在多个有限关系结构上对标记对象进行一致分类。尽管每个候选公式可以被精确评估，但量化一阶公式构成巨大的搜索空间，且LLM输出常常在语义上有前景但并非完全正确，需要一种将精确验证与LLM生成能力相结合的框架。

Method: Hypothesis Frontier是一个验证器引导的神经符号框架：每一轮对LLM生成的每个公式在所有训练对象上进行精确评估，保留最强验证假设，并利用剩余错误指导后续生成；符号处理用于修复无效公式并保持与LLM生成假设的对齐，同时简化训练-验证公式而不改变任何训练预测；最终公式选定后进行精确简化。

Result: 在匹配模型、问题集和LLM轮次预算的条件下，Hypothesis Frontier解决的问题数量显著多于重复原始提示生成；最终公式选定后的精确简化在不改变训练预测的前提下缩短了许多训练-验证公式。

Conclusion: 精确符号推理不仅有助于解决更多归纳问题，还能压缩许多最终得到的公式，展示了神经符号方法在一阶概念合成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: First-order concept synthesis asks a system to infer one formula that classifies labeled objects consistently across several finite relational structures. Every candidate can be evaluated exactly, but quantified first-order formulas form a vast search space, and LLM outputs are often semantically promising without being fully correct. We introduce Hypothesis Frontier, a verifier-guided neurosymbolic framework that evaluates each LLM formula on every training object, retains the strongest verified hypothesis across rounds, and uses its remaining errors to guide subsequent generation. Symbolic processing repairs invalid formulas while remaining anchored to the LLM-generated hypothesis, and simplifies train-valid formulas without changing any training prediction. Under matched models, problem sets, and LLM-round budgets, Hypothesis Frontier solves substantially more problems than repeated original-prompt generation. After the final formulas are selected, exact simplification shortens many train-valid formulas while preserving every training prediction. Exact symbolic reasoning therefore helps both to solve more induction problems and to compress many of the resulting formulas.

</details>


### [185] [Enhanced Filtering Algorithms for the Euclidean Traveling Salesperson Problem and its variants in Constraint Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10881)
*Alessandro Bertagnon,Marco Gavanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧几里得旅行商问题（TSP），提出利用坐标几何信息的新滤波算法，在约束逻辑编程中实现比传统完整距离矩阵方法更强的约束传播，并将方法论扩展到欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP），实验证明其计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束规划方法处理欧几里得TSP时，仅计算完整距离矩阵并将其视为一般性问题，忽略了节点坐标所携带的几何信息。这种做法限制了约束传播的强度，未能充分利用问题的空间结构。

Method: 提出新的滤波算法并在约束逻辑编程（CLP）中实现，通过直接利用点的坐标信息来进行更强的约束传播，而非仅依赖预计算的完整距离矩阵。该方法被进一步扩展到其他欧几里得TSP变体，包括EGTSP。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在计算效率上优于现有方法，展现出明显的计算优势。

Conclusion: 利用几何信息可以在约束规划中实现比传统方法更强的约束传播，该思路对包括EGTSP在内的欧几里得TSP变体均有效，具有实际路由与物流应用价值。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [186] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Combodied Agents"（联合具身智能体）范式，将数字智能体与具身智能体统一为以人为中心的闭环系统，以人类个体状态轨迹为核心建模、干预与评估对象，通过事件感知、纵向可修正记忆、个人世界模型和可采性干预策略，实现从外部任务执行转向持续的人类福祉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI存在结构性缺口：数字智能体仅转换软件状态，具身智能体仅转换物理状态，两者都无法建模、预测和干预人的动态状态与能动性（例如老人漏服药物时，系统无法判断其是否遗忘、困惑、有副作用或故意拒绝，也无法决定何种支持合适）。

Method: 提出Combodied Agents范式，形成闭环：事件级多模态感知重建有意义的个人事件；纵向可修正记忆提供时间上下文；个人世界模型（Personal World Models）在替代决策与干预下估计未来个人状态与结果；可采性干预策略在同意、不确定性、安全、可逆性与用户控制约束下选择成比例支持。系统利用软件工具、传感器、可穿戴设备、机器人与人类服务作为行动通道，而非最终目标；人与环境的反馈持续更新闭环。框架采用有界用途、不确定性感知、用户可修正的表征，而非穷尽的数字孪生。

Result: 统一了个人助理、健康智能体、AI陪伴与自适应人机系统的碎片化能力；按人类状态目标、关系情境与智能体角色组织设计空间；提出以场景为中心的评估、能动性保持度量、基准需求、边缘原生个人模型与治理方向。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents将Agentic AI从外部任务完成转向持续的人类利益，标志着从"以任务为中心"到"以人为中心"的范式转移，为下一代人机协同智能体提供了统一的理论框架与设计方向。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [187] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IO Factory，一个用于模拟信息与影响力行动（影响行动）的AI驱动框架。该框架将影响行动建模为从规划、平台行动、曝光、解释、测量到适应的完整连续过程，在受控模拟平台上连接行动者角色、平台行为、曝光记录、模型评估与群体变化。系统支持多达10万智能体的配置规模，可生成可检查的曝光证据与信念变量变化数据，为可复现研究和红队分析提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操纵威胁已从单个语言模型生成的具有说服力的文本，演变为AI蜂群（AI swarms）——即由协调智能体组成的持久群体，能够适应平台反馈并将有组织的行动伪装成普通社交互动。此类行动无法从孤立的单条消息中识别，必须在规划、平台行动、曝光、解释、测量和适应的连续谱系中进行分析，因此需要端到端可追踪的模拟框架。

Method: IO Factory在受控模拟平台内表示完整的影响行动过程，将行动者角色、平台行为、曝光记录、结构化基于模型的评估以及模拟群体中的配置变化相互关联。作者实现了该架构，并在多达100,000个智能体的配置上进行了评估。

Result: 结果表明，IO Factory能够在规模上执行影响行动时间线，并生成可检查的曝光证据和配置信念变量的可测量变化。通过记录每次运行中的行动者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径和测量规则，该框架支持可复现的研究。

Conclusion: IO Factory为AI蜂群式协调影响行动的模拟与红队分析提供了一种可复现、可溯源、可扩展的解决方案，弥补了从单条消息级分析到大规模在线影响行动全链路模拟之间的空白。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [188] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkRetrieve是一个测试时扩展框架，通过在推理过程中动态检索并注入已解决的示例来提升大型推理模型（LRM）的推理性能，在五个推理模型和四个基准上取得了最高60%的相对准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过增加推理时计算量来生成更长的思维链以提高性能，但近期研究表明，顺序式的测试时扩展往往带来收益递减甚至负面效果，因为更长的推理轨迹会出现不确定性增加、错误累积和偏离原始问题等问题。

Method: ThinkRetrieve框架在LRM推理的每个中间步骤，从外部语料库中动态检索与当前状态相关的已解决问题及其逐步解答，并将这些示例直接注入思维轨迹中，为模型提供如何推理的指导而非仅仅是何种事实相关。

Result: 在五个推理模型（1.5B-8B参数）上，在GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025和SciQ四个基准上的实验表明，ThinkRetrieve相比标准测试时扩展策略持续提升准确率，在AIME 2025上相对提升高达60%。

Conclusion: ThinkRetrieve通过逐步骤检索并注入已解决示例为推理过程提供过程性指导，有效缓解了长思维链中错误累积和推理漂移的问题，展示了推理时检索增强相对于单纯增加推理计算量的优势。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [189] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FedCGR框架，将联邦跨域推荐重新定义为基于稳定语义物品语言的生成任务，通过共享的离散语义ID词表实现跨域对齐，并结合可靠性感知语义接口注入本地协同过滤信号，以及原型个性化生成器选择性聚合共享参数以缓解异构性带来的负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦部署下的跨域推荐面临核心矛盾：跨域对齐通常依赖重叠用户或共享交互信号等行为锚点，但这些锚点在联邦场景中稀疏、不可得或涉及隐私。传统方法无法在不交换私有交互或对齐域特定嵌入的前提下实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Method: 1) 将物品表示为基于公共物品侧元数据的离散语义ID（SID）序列，通过共享词表实现跨域物品对齐，避免交换私有交互；2) 保持SID tokenizer固定以确保跨客户端的token一致性；3) 通过可靠性感知语义接口（reliability-aware semantic interface）注入本地CF证据，解决语义瓶颈问题；4) 训练原型个性化生成器（prototype-personalized generator），根据域间相关性选择性聚合共享参数，同时保持域特定参数本地化，避免标准联邦平均在域异构下的负迁移。

Result: 在六个Amazon跨域场景的实验表明，FedCGR一致优于联邦生成式基线，并在全排序和采样评估协议下，与强顺序推荐方法和联邦CDR方法达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: FedCGR验证了将联邦CDR重构为语义物品语言上的生成任务的有效性，即在保持物品语言稳定的同时显式进行适应，平衡了跨域对齐、隐私保护和联邦异构性之间的矛盾，为联邦跨域推荐提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [190] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XCoT-VLA提出了一种用紧凑可执行的XCoT token替代冗长自然语言CoT的VLA模型，通过Reason-Action监督自动构建、确定性token路由和XCPO优化，在保持实时规划预算的同时显著提升自动驾驶轨迹预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽能连接场景理解与轨迹生成，但传统自然语言CoT存在开放性、解码成本高、难以作为行动导向表示进行优化等问题，不适合实时控制场景。

Method: 1) 用自动构建的Reason-Action监督学习紧凑可执行的XCoT token替代冗长CoT；2) XCoT序列保持在上下文环境中，通过共享多模态自注意力条件化固定轨迹查询；3) 确定性token-function路由：Reason FFN处理XCoT token，Control FFN处理轨迹查询以进行flow-matching轨迹生成；4) 提出XCPO作为同token空间的可选优化扩展。

Result: 在通用分布集上纵向ADE从1.645降至1.323，变道场景横向FDE从1.616降至0.648；仅用2-6个可执行XCoT token即可大幅降低自回归推理开销，保持在实时规划预算内。

Conclusion: 驾驶导向推理可以是紧凑、可执行且直接连接轨迹生成的，XCoT-VLA验证了该范式在实时自动驾驶规划和性能上的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [191] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: V-FiLLM是一个从可执行计算树自动生成金融推理基准的框架，通过符号化求值获取ground truth，无需任何模型参与标注即可任意规模生成数据。评估发现推理深度增加时准确率下降高达51%，对抗性数值扰动下下降47%；轻量级LoRA微调可将准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，并在FinQA上超越基线5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在STEM领域对LLM的评估已有显著进展，但基于结构化数据的金融推理评估仍相对匮乏。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个可扩展、无标注成本且不继承生成器错误率的金融推理基准生成框架。

Method: V-FiLLM从真实表格出发构建可执行计算树，通过符号求值获得ground truth，并将树渲染为自然语言问题。框架暴露四个独立可控的难度轴：计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文大小。评估中测试了开源模型在不同难度下的表现，并使用LoRA在验证过的思维链轨迹上进行微调。

Result: 推理深度增加时准确率最高下降51%；对抗性数值扰动下准确率下降高达47个百分点；LoRA微调使准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，并在FinQA上比基础模型高出5个百分点。

Conclusion: V-FiLLM展示了针对性的低成本适配（LoRA微调）是提升金融表格组合推理能力的有前景方向，同时揭示了现有开源模型在鲁棒金融推理上的显著挑战。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [192] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip是一种无需评估的技能压缩方法，通过寻找最短的忠实结构解释来压缩自演化智能体中积累的可复用技能，具有一次性模式和持续Zip-on-Write模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化智能体持续积累可复用技能，但技能会因重复规则、复制粘贴的多余动作序列而变得冗余，导致注入成本高、维护困难。通用提示压缩不适用于技能压缩，因为技能不是平坦文本，具有名称/描述（何时适用）、工作流（如何执行）、工具/输出契约（有效性约束）和罕见异常（关键例外）等结构化特性。基于评估的压缩虽能测试这些行为，但引入了 rollout、成本和对压缩时评估集的依赖。

Method: SkillZip提出"解释一次，多处引用"的直觉：在适用作用域内只陈述一次重复规则，将重复动作序列抽象为共享过程，仅保留差异作为显式例外。该方法将此直觉形式化为类型化最小描述长度（MDL）目标，作用于技能契约和残差，并对每个提取的触发器、工作流边、工具需求、义务和输出字段施加硬覆盖约束。支持两种模式：一次性模式（单次结构化提取调用+确定性优化）和持续Zip-on-Write模式（整合每次自我演化补丁，无需重放任务或重新解析完整历史）。

Result: 通过综合实验评估，SkillZip在压缩性能、泛化能力和成本开销方面均展现出有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SkillZip通过结构化MDL目标实现了无需压缩时评估即可高效压缩技能的能力，具有简单的共享阈值、按构造保留唯一罕见规则，并支持高效局部更新，是一种适用于自演化智能体技能的实用压缩方案。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [193] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个轨道交通站点知识图谱数据集RTSKG，通过统一模式显式建模城市中不同实体（站点、路段、兴趣点等）之间的空间与语义交互，以支持城市级轨道交通站点相关任务（如客流预测、商业推荐），并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级轨道交通站点相关任务需要大规模城市数据，但现有研究在数据组织层面往往忽略了多种城市实体之间的复杂交互关系，缺乏一个能够系统建模这些交互的数据基础。

Method: 构建Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG)数据集，设计统一的模式（schema），将轨道交通站点、道路路段、兴趣点等异构城市实体整合到知识图谱中，并以链接数据（Linked Data）形式发布，提供可访问的URI。

Result: 在站点周边商户推荐和知识增强客流预测两个任务上的评估结果表明RTSKG是有效的，证明了其支持城市级轨道交通站点分析的潜力。

Conclusion: RTSKG作为首个面向城市级轨道交通站点任务的知识图谱数据集，弥补了现有研究在城市实体交互建模方面的不足，为相关任务提供了结构化、可复用的数据基础设施。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [194] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了agentic编程README（如CLAUDE.md）中提示词的无界增长问题，将其命名为"灾难性记忆"（catastrophic remembering，与灾难性遗忘相对）。作者在1,867个仓库的247,694条指令生命周期中验证了该现象，并发现通过在提示词中编写注释（prompt comments）可以显著阻止这种增长：在可验证场景下减少99.3%的冗余指令，在真实场景下将agentic指令遵循能力提升高达23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实仓库中的agentic README（如CLAUDE.md）会无界增长，直到仓库退役或被人整体重写。作者追溯其根本原因是"不完美回忆"：追加一条指令总是廉价，但一旦指令背后的理由丢失，删除它需要付出O(2^|D|)的代价来评估是否会引发正确性回归。这种发散与持续学习中组织的"灾难性遗忘"形成镜像，作者称之为"灾难性记忆"。

Method: （1）大规模实证分析：在1,867个真实仓库中追踪247,694条指令的生命周期，量化提示词增长速率、净增指令数和删除概率的log-hazard；（2）提出提示词注释（prompt comments）作为缓解手段：将IFEval求逆，构造"可验证世界"（verifiable worlds）使最优提示词已知，在其中验证注释编码潜在推理（latent reasoning）能否阻止增长；（3）将相同的求逆方法应用于WildIFEval，在真实世界的agentic指令遵循场景中测试提示词注释的效果。

Result: （1）agentic提示词在生命周期内无界增长，平均增长+226%（超过三倍），每提交净增+4.9条指令；指令越老，被删除的可能性越低（log-hazard为-0.032/提交）。（2）在可验证世界中，编码潜在推理的注释移除了99.3%的冗余指令，使增长率从+211.3%降至+1.4%。（3）在WildIFEval上，提示词注释使真实世界的agentic指令遵循性能提升高达23.1%。

Conclusion: 作者提出一个核心类比："如果英语是新的代码，为什么我们还没有注释？"——正如代码中注释记录了设计理由从而允许安全重构，提示词注释同样能编码指令的潜在推理，使删除旧指令变得安全可行，从而阻止提示词的灾难性记忆。这为agentic提示词工程开辟了可持续维护的新方向。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [195] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 sLTN（结构逻辑张量网络），将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为一阶公民引入逻辑张量网络（LTN），使其能够直接表达时间、序列和关系约束。sLTN 在不含结构维度时退化为原始 LTN，并提供了基于 PyTorch 的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 LTN 仅适用于扁平个体集合的数据表示，无法显式捕捉时间顺序、序列位置或图连接等结构组织信息，限制了其在时序与结构化推理任务中的应用。

Method: 在 LTN 中引入结构维度作为命名张量轴，使其成为语言的一等元素；支持对结构维度的显式量化，通过结构关系表达约束，并在逻辑层面直接刻画时间、序列与关系语义。形式化定义了 sLTN 的语法与模糊张量语义，并论证了无结构维度时退化为原始 LTN 语义。基于声明式签名、公式解析和张量解释实现了 PyTorch 框架。

Result: 在代表性的时间和序列推理示例上验证了 sLTN 的可行性，并作为 sltn 库（https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn）的配套论文发布。

Conclusion: sLTN 将结构信息以本征方式融入神经符号推理，扩展了 LTN 的表达能力，为需结合时间、序列或图结构的推理任务提供了统一且可微的框架。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [196] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对Grothendieck常数$K_G$界改进的深入案例研究，展示了AI系统如何在数学研究中产生专家认可的新颖见解，并讨论了AI数学研究的优势、劣势及创造理想条件的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多地被用于数学研究，但如何有效使用它们仍不清楚。作者希望通过一个具体的高影响力数学问题来系统性地评估AI在真实数学研究中的潜力与局限，为数学界使用AI提供实践指导。

Method: 作者以一个AI研究系统为核心，针对Grothendieck常数$K_G$的已知边界进行改进，将下界改进为$6\pi/11$，上界改进为$\pi/(2\log(1+\sqrt2))-10^{-4}$。文中详细记录了使用AI进行数学研究的全过程经验。

Result: 利用AI系统成功将Grothendieck常数$K_G$的已知最紧边界收紧为$6\pi/11 \le K_G \le \pi/(2\log(1+\sqrt2))-10^{-4}$，且AI得到的见解被领域专家视为新颖突破。

Conclusion: AI在数学研究中可以产生专家认可的新颖见解并带来实质性的数学进展，但其有效使用需要特定的理想条件。作者提供了详细的实践经验，为未来在数学研究中使用AI提供了参考框架。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>


### [197] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对六大开发者的六个前沿语言模型进行了行为可操控性评估，使用300对配对基线与操控项（涵盖价值观冲突、推理激发、推理抑制三个类别）及40个验证项，通过24,480个盲审同行判断（留一法共识评分）发现：模型不仅在操控偏移量上存在差异，更在响应模式上存在质性差异，部分模式仅出现在个别模型中。GPT-5在拒绝披露推理过程的同时保持答案完整（99% vs 其他模型0%）；Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。对开放权重模型Llama的内部机制追踪表明，线性探针可从残差流以0.87的留出准确率解码该行为，且注入该方向可将行为从0%驱动至86%。所有发现在token预算修正和假设盲评控制实验下均保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型采用不同的训练数据、目标和安全管线训练，但这些差异是否会在显式操控压力下产生可测量的行为差异尚未得到充分探索。该研究旨在填补这一空白，系统性地量化和对比不同模型在受控条件下的行为可操控性。

Method: 选取六个开发者的六个前沿模型，使用300对配对基线与操控项（分为价值观冲突、推理激发、推理抑制三类）外加40个验证项进行评估。所有六个模型充当盲审同行评审，依据固定行为评分标准对每个响应分类，产生24,480个判断并由留一法共识打分。同时采用线性探针从残差流解码行为，并通过干预扫描（方向注入）验证因果性。所有实验配合token预算修正和假设盲评控制实验。

Result: 模型在操控下不仅在行为偏移量上不同，更在响应模式上存在质性差异：GPT-5在拒绝推理披露的同时保持答案完整（99% vs 其他模型0%）；Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。对Llama的线性探针达到0.87留出准确率，方向注入将行为从0%驱动至86%。所有发现在两种控制条件下均保持成立。

Conclusion: 不同模型在显式操控压力下表现出质性不同的行为模式，这些差异可追溯到模型内部表征（残差流中的可解码方向），且具有因果可控性。该发现对理解模型的训练差异与行为可操控性之间的关系，以及安全评估中的跨模型泛化具有重要启示。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models). Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5 resist explicit suppression instructions and in different ways. Using Llama as the open-weight model, we trace the largest behavioral split to its internals. A linear probe decodes the behavior from the residual stream at 0.87 held-out accuracy while injecting that direction during generation drives the behavior from 0% to 86% across an intervention sweep. Every finding holds under both a token-budget remediation and a control experiment with a hypothesis-blind judgment prompt.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-IV: Developmental Topology Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09997)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TGO-IV，一个基于持续同调的拓扑框架，用于分析Transformer表示在层间演化过程中的全局拓扑特征变化，从而揭示原始输入表示何时以及如何逐步演化为任务相关的特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer可解释性研究主要分析孤立层或整个网络的表示，而对单个表示及其流形在各层之间的发展演化过程缺乏探索。本文旨在通过拓扑视角填补这一空白，理解原始输入表示何时、如何转化为任务相关的特征表示。

Method: TGO-IV基于持续同调（Persistent Homology）构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形，从token级别的表示点云中提取拓扑持久签名。框架包含多种互补的拓扑观测工具：持久性图（Persistence Diagrams）、条形图（Barcode Diagrams）、Betti曲线（Betti Curves）、持久性景观（Persistence Landscapes）、瓶颈距离（Bottleneck Distance）和Wasserstein距离，以全面分析表示点云在前向传播过程中的全局拓扑演化。

Result: 该框架提供了对Transformer表示跨层演化的全面拓扑分析，能够识别表示从原始输入到任务相关特征转变的关键层或趋势，弥补了现有研究在表示流形层间演化方面的空白。

Conclusion: TGO-IV通过引入拓扑数据分析方法，为理解Transformer如何学习提供了新的视角和工具，从全局拓扑而非局部几何的角度揭示了表示在层间的演化规律。

Abstract: Transformers have had a profound impact on the world of language processing and computer vision. As efforts to answer the million-dollar question of ``How does a Transformer learn?" have been increasing, existing interpretability studies primarily analyze representations at isolated layers or the network as a whole, while the developmental evolution of individual representations and its manifolds across transformer layers remains underexplored. With this work, we aim at providing a comprehensive analysis of the evolution of representations as the representation point cloud transforms across the layers; thereby attempting to isolate layers or establish a trend which comes closer to justifying how and when raw input representations evolve into task-relevant feature representations. Thus, Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV introduces a topological framework for analysing the evolution of Transformer representations through the lens of Persistent Homology. Rather than studying local geometric properties alone, TGO-IV constructs Vietoris--Rips simplicial complexes from token-level representation point clouds and investigates the evolution of their persistent topological signatures across Transformer layers. The proposed framework comprises complementary topological observatories including Persistence Diagrams, Barcode Diagrams, Betti Curves, Persistence Landscapes, Bottleneck Distance, and Wasserstein Distance, enabling a comprehensive analysis of how the global topology of representation point clouds develops throughout the forward pass.

</details>


### [199] [Uncertainty-Aware Ensemble Deep Randomized Neural Networks for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10007)
*M. Sajid,A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,P. N. Suganthan,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于直觉模糊理论的深度随机向量功能链接网络框架（IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL），通过联合考虑隶属度与非隶属度对训练样本进行自适应加权，以区分干净样本、噪声和离群点，提升模型在含噪声真实数据集上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA深度随机神经网络（如dRVFL和edRVFL）对所有训练样本一视同仁（统一权重），这在包含噪声和离群点的真实数据上鲁棒性不足；同时污染特征在隐藏层间的传播会损害模型的决策能力。因此需要一种能够区分干净、噪声和离群样本的机制来增强模型鲁棒性。

Method: 提出IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL框架，将直觉模糊理论统一引入深度RVFL模型。具体包括：(1)在核空间中利用样本邻域信息，同时计算每个样本的隶属度（基于样本到所属类别质心的距离）和非隶属度（量化样本在局部邻域内的异质性）；(2)综合隶属度与非隶属度为训练样本分配自适应权重，实现对干净样本、噪声和离群点的有效区分。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上（含/不含高斯噪声两种设置）进行了大量实验，结果表明所提出的IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL模型优于现有的SOTA模糊与非模糊方法。源代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过直觉模糊理论与深度RVFL模型的结合，有效解决了深度随机网络对所有样本统一加权而导致的鲁棒性不足问题，为噪声和离群点场景下的深度随机神经网络提供了更具鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: The current state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep randomized neural networks, such as deep Random Vector Functional Link (dRVFL) and ensemble deep RVFL (edRVFL), treat all training samples uniformly, which limits their robustness and effectiveness when applied to real-world datasets containing noise and outliers. Furthermore, the propagation of contaminated features across hidden layers negatively influences the decision-making capability of these models. To overcome these limitations, we propose intuitionistic fuzzy dRVFL (IF-dRVFL) and intuitionistic fuzzy edRVFL (IF-edRVFL) frameworks that enhance model robustness. The proposed models unify intuitionistic fuzzy theory to exploit sample neighborhood information in the kernel space by jointly considering membership and non-membership degrees for each sample. Membership degrees are computed based on the distance of samples from their respective class centroids, while non-membership degrees quantify sample heterogeneity within local neighborhoods. These measures are employed to assign adaptive weights to training samples, enabling effective discrimination among clean, noisy, and outlier data points. Extensive experiments conducted on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, with and without the presence of Gaussian noise, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed IF-dRVFL and IF-edRVFL models over existing SOTA fuzzy and non-fuzzy approaches. The source code is available at https://github.com/mtanveer1/IF-edRVFL.

</details>


### [200] [CurveFP: Rational-Radix Logarithmic Datatypes with Closed Products for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10010)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CurveFP，一种封闭乘积码本家族，通过交错对数曲线分布量化幅值，在紧凑块尺度下实现精确的符号异或和整数索引更新的乘积运算。CurveFP七位在四个7B-9B模型上优于张量级FP8困惑度，CurveFP八位在所有36个正反GEMM比较中降低操作数NMSE，并在3B token预训练中实现更低格式诱导惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度数据类型可降低语言模型成本，但大多数格式仅优化标量保真度，未改变其乘积所引入的算术精度损失。现有FP8等格式在乘积运算中缺乏代数封闭性，导致计算路径复杂且精度受限。

Method: CurveFP通过交错的数条对数曲线在紧凑块尺度下分布量化幅值，使用有理基数调节动态范围与局部分辨率，均匀曲线索引保证所有非零乘积的代数封闭性。乘积运算化简为精确的符号异或与整数索引更新，并由导出有限相位计数决定累加调度。实例化为CurveFP八（E4C3/E5C2）用于训练和CurveFP七（E3C3）用于紧凑部署。

Result: CurveFP七位在四个7B-9B模型上以少一个元素位的代价优于张量级FP8困惑度，保持在原生质量的1.32%以内。CurveFP八位在所有36个配对前后向GEMM比较中降低操作数NMSE。在三个匹配的128.3M参数三联体上，CurveFP八位达到平均BF16推理困惑度22.5366（FP8为22.5407），三个种子中均产生更低格式诱导惩罚。36格下游矩阵中，CurveFP八训练检查点在全部12个种子-格式比较中取得更低WikiText-103困惑度。

Conclusion: CurveFP建立了同时兼顾FP8级数值行为、七位推理和显著简化乘积路径的算术协同设计，验证了封闭乘积代数在低精度语言模型训练与推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Low-precision datatypes reduce language-model cost, but most formats optimize scalar fidelity while leaving the arithmetic induced by their products unchanged. We introduce CurveFP, a closed-product codebook family that distributes quantized magnitudes across interleaved logarithmic curves under compact block scales. A rational radix tunes dynamic range against local resolution, while uniform curve indices make every nonzero product algebraically closed. Product formation becomes an exact sign XOR and integer-index update, and a derived finite phase count determines the accumulation schedule. We instantiate this algebra as CurveFP eight E4C3/E5C2 for training and CurveFP seven E3C3 for compact deployment. In evaluation, CurveFP seven beats tensor-wise FP8 perplexity on four 7B--9B models with one fewer element bit and stays within 1.32\% of native quality. CurveFP eight lowers operand NMSE in all 36 paired forward and backward GEMM comparisons. Across three matched 128.3M-parameter triplets, every mode completes 3B-token pretraining per seed; CurveFP eight reaches mean BF16-inference perplexity 22.5366 versus 22.5407 for FP8 and incurs a lower format-induced penalty in all three seeds. A 36-cell downstream matrix finds lower WikiText-103 perplexity for the CurveFP eight-trained checkpoints in all 12 seed-format comparisons, with mixed PG-19 and task deltas. Together, these results establish CurveFP as an arithmetic co-design that combines FP8-class numerical behavior, seven-bit inference, and a substantially simpler product path.

</details>


### [201] [Sheaf-Based Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10016)
*Gabriele D'Acunto,Enrico Grimaldi,Valeria Avino,Mario Edoardo Pandolfo,Leonardo Di Nino,Sergio Barbarossa,Paolo Di Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL)，一种建立在可学习层片限制映射上的流形约束几何对齐框架，通过层片拉普拉斯二次粘合正则项实现异构联邦系统中无需共享全局潜空间的表示对齐，并提出交替优化的分布式算法Sheaf-FRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦系统中各智能体在数据分布、感知模态、模型架构、潜空间维度及本地学习目标上存在显著差异，需要让智能体在缺乏共享潜空间的前提下学习并交换有信息量的表示，而现有方法大多假定存在共享全局潜空间，无法适应真实异构场景。

Method: SFRL在联合优化本地目标函数的同时，引入基于可学习层片限制映射的流形约束几何对齐正则项。全局一致性通过正交变换与等距嵌入对齐相邻潜空间表示而涌现，而非依赖共享潜空间。对齐由层片拉普拉斯诱导的二次粘合正则项强制实现，可学习的限制映射自适应调整几何结构以匹配观测数据；该惩罚项仅在小规模共享试点样本上评估以保证可扩展性与通信效率。Sheaf-FRL算法交替进行本地模型的梯度更新与边级限制映射的闭式Procrustes更新，并在确定性与随机设定下均收敛至一阶驻点。

Result: 在语义通信的协作分类任务中，面对模型与数据异构，Sheaf-FRL在不同本地分布偏移水平下的本地及通信后分类准确率均优于基线方法，并对潜空间维度压缩表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SFRL为异构联邦学习提供了一种无需共享潜空间的几何表示对齐范式，通过可学习层片结构实现全局一致性涌现，具备良好的收敛保证、通信效率和维度压缩鲁棒性，为异构联邦系统及语义通信场景提供了有前景的通用框架。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated systems require agents to learn and exchange informative representations despite differences in data distributions, sensing modalities, model architectures, latent dimensionalities, and local learning objectives. To address this challenge, we propose Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL), a general framework that jointly optimizes local objectives with a manifold-constrained geometric alignment regularizer based on learnable sheaf restriction maps. Unlike most existing approaches, SFRL does not assume a shared global latent space. Instead, global consistency emerges from the alignment of neighboring latent representations through orthogonal transformations and isometric embeddings. This alignment is enforced by a quadratic gluing regularizer induced by the sheaf Laplacian, whose learnable restriction maps adapt the geometry to the observed data.
