  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程控制的时空动态系统建模面临两大挑战：基于物理的仿真器计算成本高昂（需要迭代数值求解），而纯数据驱动模型依赖大量训练数据且对动态工况泛化能力差。需要一种既能生成物理一致训练数据又提高数据效率的方法。

Method: DEFT是一种频域数据采样方法：识别物理系统的主导傅里叶模态，系统性地变动对应的幅度和相位，然后通过逆离散傅里叶变换生成物理一致的训练数据。此外，论文推导了该方法的泛化界，并提供了理论上选择主导模态数K的原则性准则。

Result: 三组实验验证：（1）在经典PDE求解中，当系统由少数显著频率分量主导时优于传统方法；（2）在PDEBench的扩散-吸附和Burgers方程上作为数据价值过滤器，减少40%数据需求而精度损失小于2%；（3）在电池退化PDE系统中R²超过0.99，且频域特征可迁移至其他电池化学体系，仅需20%微调数据。

Conclusion: DEFT是一种高效的算子学习数据采样方法，能显著降低数据需求、提升预测精度并支持跨体系迁移，为PDE驱动的动态系统建模提供了理论与实验双重验证的有效方案。

Abstract: Modeling spatiotemporal dynamical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) poses two major challenges: it either requires expensive physics-based simulators that entail iterative numerical solving at high computational cost, or it depends on abundant training data, yet purely data-driven models often generalize poorly to downstream dynamic operating conditions. We propose DEFT, a frequency-domain data sampling method that identifies the dominant Fourier modes of a physical system and systematically varies the corresponding amplitudes and phases to generate physically consistent training data via the inverse discrete Fourier transform. In addition, we derive a generalization bound of this method. We note that it also provides a theoretically principled criterion for selecting $K$. We evaluate the proposed method through three sets of experiments, each targeting a distinct aspect of its utility. First, we validate the framework on canonical PDEs solving demonstrating that it outperforms traditional methods when the system is dominated by a few prominent frequency components. Second, we employ DEFT as a data-value filter on the diffusion--sorption and Burgers equations of PDEBench, showing that it reduces data requirements by $40\%$ while sacrificing less than $2\%$ in predictive accuracy. Third, to evaluate DEFT for more challenging and practically relevant problems, we validate it in the battery degradation PDE system, achieving consistently high predictive accuracy across various test datasets with $R^2$ values exceeding $0.99$. Moreover, the learned frequency-domain features transfer to other battery chemistries with only $20\%$ of the fine-tuning data. These results demonstrate that DEFT is an effective data-sampling method for efficient operator learning.

</details>


### [269] [Derivative Computation in PINNs: Automatic Differentiation, Finite Differences and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11020)
*Maciej J. Mikulski,Tadeusz Uhl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了物理信息神经网络（PINNs）中有限差分（FD）作为自动微分（AD）替代方案的计算方法。在三个基准PDE上，通过适当校准步长，FD在精度上与AD相当，但在所有测试的批大小范围内运行更快且显著减少GPU内存；提出的随机变体在平稳问题上优于AD。此外，对于具有样本间依赖性的神经网络架构（如BatchNorm、自注意力），标准PyTorch autograd方法存在静默错误，而正确的逐样本替代方案在PINN相关批大小下计算不可行，FD提供的前向近似在经验上比真实逐样本导数接近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs通常依赖自动微分（AD）计算损失中的PDE导数项，但AD在训练中引入了显著的计算和内存开销。作者希望探索有限差分（FD）作为替代方案，以提升训练效率并解决AD在具有样本间依赖性的架构中面临的正确性问题。

Method: 在三个基准PDE上系统对比FD与AD的精度、运行速度和GPU内存消耗；提出一种随机FD变体；针对具有样本间依赖性的架构（BatchNorm、自注意力），揭示标准PyTorch autograd的静默错误，并利用FD提供前向近似。

Result: 校准步长后FD在所有基准问题上精度与AD相当，在全部测试批大小范围内运行更快且内存占用显著更少；随机FD变体在平稳问题上优于AD；对于样本间依赖架构，FD的前向近似比标准方法更接近真实逐样本导数约一个数量级。

Conclusion: FD是PINNs中AD的可行且高效的替代方案，不仅计算开销更低，还能规避AD在特定架构下的静默错误问题，值得在实际PINN应用中广泛采用。

Abstract: We systematically investigate finite-difference (FD) derivative computation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) as an alternative to automatic differentiation (AD). On three benchmark PDEs we show that, with a properly calibrated step size, FD matches AD in accuracy on every problem while running faster across the full tested batch-size range and using substantially less GPU memory, and that a stochastic variant we propose outperforms AD on a stationary problem. We further show that for neural architectures with inter-sample dependencies (e.g. BatchNorm, self-attention) the standard PyTorch autograd idiom is silently incorrect; the correct per-sample alternative is computationally infeasible at PINN-relevant batch sizes, while FD provides a forward-only approximation that is empirically an order of magnitude closer to the true per-sample derivative.

</details>


### [270] [Mapping and Measuring the Behavioral Evolution of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11027)
*Dong Qiao,Chris Ding,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一套无需标签的模型行为分析框架，对六个模型家族共32个模型在10K共享提示上的输出进行嵌入式表征，提出三种互补的句级不相似度量（对齐逐提示平均距离、PCA压缩的不一致摘要、免对齐的Gromov–Wasserstein差异），从静态组织与时间演变两个维度刻画模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准排行榜只能说明模型"表现多好"，却无法回答模型行为之间如何关联、跨代际如何演变。作者希望超越单一性能指标，系统刻画模型输出行为的结构与演化规律。

Method: 1) 用共享的10K提示库收集32个模型的响应并嵌入；2) 构建三种句级不相似度量：对齐平均逐提示距离（伪度量）、PCA压缩的逐提示分歧、免对齐的Gromov–Wasserstein差异；3) 通过行为地图、家族漂移、层次聚类、跨家族收敛与响应云离散度进行研究；4) 用token级MMD做交叉验证；5) 建立测度论框架并给出架构无关的充分条件（行为相似性与推理提示覆盖、小额外群体对数损失、相近有效目标分布相关联）。

Result: 模型家族形成一致聚类，gpt-2为全局离群点；跨家族距离随时间减小；近期推理导向模型响应云较紧凑；token级MMD与句级平均距离高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.98）并复现相同定性结论；三种额外编码器（最小达73倍缩小）保持秩几何、离群点与时间趋势符号。

Conclusion: 模型行为存在清晰的家族化组织与跨代趋同趋势；该无标签分析管道对不同编码器鲁棒，可作为基准排行榜的补充工具，并给出一个训练侧的理论充分条件来解释观察到的行为趋同趋势。

Abstract: Benchmark leaderboards summarize how well a language model performs, but not how its behavior relates to that of other models or changes across generations. We characterize the output behavior of 32 models from six families using their responses to a shared bank of 10{,}000 prompts. After embedding each response, we construct three complementary sentence-level dissimilarities: an aligned mean per-prompt distance, which is a pseudometric on observed model responses; a PCA-compressed summary of prompt-wise disagreement; and an alignment-free Gromov--Wasserstein discrepancy between models' internal response geometries. We use these constructions to study static organization and temporal change on a release-date axis through behavioral maps, family-wise drift, hierarchical clustering, cross-family convergence, and response-cloud dispersion. Across the three constructions, model families form coherent clusters, with \texttt{gpt-2} as a global outlier; cross-family distances decrease over time; and several recent reasoning-oriented models have comparatively compact response clouds. A token-level cross-check based on per-prompt Maximum Mean Discrepancy closely agrees with the sentence-level mean distance (Spearman $ρ=0.98$) and recovers the same qualitative findings. We organize these comparisons through a measure-theoretic lens making their alignment and invariance assumptions explicit. We also establish an architecture-agnostic sufficient condition linking behavioral similarity to inference-prompt coverage, small excess population log-loss, and similar effective target distributions---a possible training-side account rather than an empirical explanation of the observed trends. Our pipeline is label-free, and re-encoding every response with three further encoders---down to one $73\times$ smaller---preserves the rank geometry, the outliers, and the sign of the time trend.

</details>


### [271] [ReRound: Reconstructive Rounding to Resolve Midpoint Ambiguity in Calibration-Free LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11045)
*He-Yen Hsieh,H. T. Kung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReRound是一种面向低比特LLM量化的后训练量化方法，通过训练条件扩散模型生成权重的连续重建信号，用于消解RTN量化中位于区间中点附近权重的舍入方向歧义，从而在3-bit和4-bit量化中显著优于RTN及多种免校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准舍入到最近(RTN)量化方法在权重接近量化区间中点时存在固有的舍入方向歧义，这种歧义会导致量化误差累积，影响低比特量化后模型的精度。

Method: ReRound首先训练一个条件扩散模型，从预训练LLM权重生成低比特权重的连续重建值作为引导信号；引入容忍度(tolerance)指标衡量量化权重与区间中点的距离，落入容忍区域内的权重使用扩散重建引导量化，靠近量化边界的权重使用RTN；通过扫描容忍度参数生成多个候选量化矩阵，选择去量化后权重的前导奇异值与原始全精度权重最匹配的候选，从而确定最优容忍度。

Result: 在多个小型LLM上，ReRound在3-bit和4-bit权重量化中一致优于标准RTN；相比大量免校准方法具有更优精度，与依赖校准的方法精度相当，且完全离线运行，低比特推理阶段无额外开销。

Conclusion: ReRound代表了一种全新的低比特量化策略，不仅适用于LLM还可扩展到其他AI模型，为后训练量化中中点歧义问题提供了有效的扩散模型引导解决方案。

Abstract: ReRound (Reconstructive Rounding) is a post-training quantization method that addresses the midpoint ambiguity inherent in standard round-to-nearest (RTN) schemes when quantizing weights near the centers of quantization intervals.
  Starting from a pretrained LLM, ReRound trains a conditional diffusion model to produce continuous reconstructions of low-bit weights for the LLM. These reconstructed weights act as a guidance signal to disambiguate the rounding direction of weights located close to interval midpoints.
  To integrate this reconstruction-guided rounding with conventional RTN, ReRound introduces a tolerance metric measuring how far the quantized weight (not the final quantized integer) is away from the midpoint: quantized weights within a tolerance region around midpoints are quantized using diffusion-based reconstructions, whereas weights closer to quantization boundaries are quantized with RTN. By sweeping the tolerance parameter, ReRound generates multiple candidate quantized integer weight matrices and selects the de-quantized weight matrix candidate whose leading singular values most closely match those of the original full-precision weights. This selected candidate determines the tolerance parameter ReRound uses.
  ReRound is particularly effective for smaller LLMs. Across a range of such models, it consistently outperforms standard RTN for 3-bit and 4-bit weight quantization. ReRound achieves superior accuracy compared to an extensive set of calibration-free methods, remains competitive with calibration-dependent approaches, and operates entirely offline, introducing no additional overhead during low-bit inference.
  The ReRound strategy represents a new approach for low-bit quantization. The method applies to AI models beyond LLMs. This paper focuses on its applications to small LLMs.

</details>


### [272] [Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning for Genomics Applications: Insights from an Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11054)
*Sepideh Saran,Mahsa Ghanbari,Uwe Ohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对深度学习模型在基因组学应用中的不确定性量化（UQ）方法进行了系统性实证分析，对比了深度集成、贝叶斯神经网络和MC-dropout三种方法的可靠性，并提供了在不同基因组学场景下的适用性指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型已成为基因组学领域广泛应用的标准计算工具，但不确定性量化及其可靠性在该领域缺乏系统性的研究关注，需要实证分析来指导方法选择。

Method: 设计系统比较框架，在序列到活性模型和单细胞表达分析两个基因组学应用领域及数据模态中，对比Deep Ensembles、Bayesian Neural Networks和Monte Carlo-dropout三种UQ方法，评估其在不同场景下量化不确定性的能力，包括类不平衡和分布外数据等常见数据集特征。

Result: 贝叶斯神经网络在捕捉由强类不平衡和分布外数据引起的不确定性方面表现更好，尽管其计算成本较高；不确定性分数还可用于选择蛋白质-RNA相互作用中的高质量预测。

Conclusion: 该比较框架为基因组学中UQ方法的适用性和可靠性提供了指导，明确了不同方法的优势与局限，并展示了不确定性分数在实际应用中的额外价值。

Abstract: Deep learning models have emerged as the standard computational tool for a wide range of applications in genomics. Yet, uncertainty quantification (UQ) -- and more specifically, the reliability of different uncertainty estimates in this domain -- has received little systematic attention. This work presents an empirical analysis of UQ in deep learning models, focusing on genomics applications. In a series of experiments, we contrast Deep Ensembles, Bayesian Neural Networks, and Monte Carlo-dropout methods. We assess their ability to quantify uncertainty in different scenarios, accounting for common dataset characteristics in two genomic application areas and modalities: sequence-to-activity models, and single-cell expression analysis. Our systematic comparison framework provides guidelines for the applicability and reliability of UQ methods in genomics, highlighting their strengths and limitations in different scenarios. We show that Bayesian Neural Networks are better at capturing uncertainty caused by strong class imbalance and out-of-distribution data in genomics, despite their computational disadvantages. Moreover, we show how uncertainty scores can be used to select high-quality predictions in protein-RNA interactions.

</details>


### [273] [Batch Size or Negatives? A Selection Rule for Memory-Constrained Recommender Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11061)
*Artyom Sabitov,Daniil Volkov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定内存预算下调优采样Softmax在推荐系统中的批次大小（n）与负样本数（k）分配问题，理论分析表明n~B、k~1（即优先增大批次而非负样本）能实现最快收敛，并通过合成实验及四个真实推荐基准验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型神经推荐系统使用全词汇量的Softmax交叉熵训练时，最终分类层需O(nK)内存。采样Softmax将逻辑和梯度控制在O(nk)，但在固定预算B=nk下，是优先增大批次n还是增加负样本k这一分配策略仍不明确，本文旨在从理论和实证角度回答这一问题。

Method: 在标准光滑性和方差假设下，对固定内存约束下的采样Softmax训练进行收敛性理论分析，推导出最优的n与k分配策略（n~B, k~1），并通过可控合成实验和四个真实序列推荐基准（含MovieLens-20M）进行验证。

Result: 理论分析表明最快收敛来自n~B、k~1的分配，即应将内存预算尽可能用于更多训练样本而非更多负样本。该配置在合成及四个真实推荐基准上均实现更快的收敛速度和更好的最终推荐质量，优于同内存约束下的不平衡分配方案。

Conclusion: 为推荐系统训练的固定内存配置提供了理论与实践基础，给出了可操作规则：在计算约束下应包含尽可能多的样本（即最大化批次大小），而非增加负样本数量。

Abstract: Large-scale neural recommender systems are typically trained with a softmax cross-entropy objective over the full item vocabulary. For a typical large number of possible items $K$, the final classification layer dominates memory, requiring $O(nK)$ logits and gradients to materialize for a batch of $n$ examples. Sampled softmax reduces this cost by restricting the objective to only $k \ll K$ candidate negative items, resulting in an $O(nk)$ memory. However, for a fixed budget $B = n k$, it remains unclear whether one should prioritize larger batches or the inclusion of more negative items.
  We address this question by analyzing sampled-softmax training under a fixed memory constraint. Under standard smoothness and variance assumptions, our theoretical evidence suggests that the fastest convergence arises from an $ n \sim B, k \sim 1$ allocation. So, an actionable rule is to include as many objects as possible given computational constraints.
  Our theory is supported by controlled synthetic and synthetic and four real sequential recommendation benchmarks, including MovieLens-20M. The suggested configuration achieve faster convergence and better final recommendation quality than imbalanced alternatives within the same memory constraint. These findings provide a theoretical and empirical foundation for configuring memory during the training of recommender systems. Code, reproducibility materials, and all scripts for generating figures are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LimitedMemoryRule-BBFB

</details>


### [274] [Cross-View Feature Matching: Survey, Benchmarking, and Foundation-Model Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11093)
*Songlin Du,Xiaoyong Lu,Zeyu Wu,Xiaobo Lu,Guobao Xiao,Bin Fan,Jiayi Ma,Takeshi Ikenaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对跨视角特征匹配领域进行了系统综述，提出了涵盖特征提取、单型/多型特征匹配器、视觉基础模型方法、训练策略和鲁棒估计的结构化分类框架，并在统一协议下对代表性方法进行了公平的基准评测，同时探讨了效率、极端条件下的鲁棒性以及跨域泛化等开放挑战和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视角特征匹配领域经过十年发展，已从任务特定模型演进到统一可泛化的对应关系模型，并受视觉基础模型（VFMs）的推动取得新进展。但现有研究在问题公式化、模型架构、训练范式和评估协议上高度多样化，缺乏统一的视角来理解和比较该领域，因此亟需系统性的综述来整合知识、提炼设计原则并提供公平的基准比较。

Method: 作者提出了一个结构化分类框架（taxonomy），涵盖特征提取、单类型特征匹配器、多类型特征匹配器、基于视觉基础模型的方法、训练策略和鲁棒估计六个维度，以此构建统一分析框架。同时，作者在一致协议下对代表性最新方法进行了统一的实验基准测试，实现公平全面的性能比较，并据此提炼关键设计原则。

Result: 该综述提供了一个统一的实验基准测试结果，对代表性最新方法在一致的协议下进行了公平对比，展示了从任务特定模型到统一可泛化对应关系模型的演化趋势，并揭示了视觉基础模型在提升跨视角匹配性能方面的关键作用。

Conclusion: 该综述为理解跨视角特征匹配在视觉基础模型时代的演变、现状和未来发展提供了全面而结构化的参考，指出了效率提升、极端条件下鲁棒性增强和跨域泛化等未来研究方向，旨在推动领域向更统一、更可泛化的对应关系模型发展。

Abstract: Cross-view feature matching aims to establish reliable correspondences across images with large viewpoint variations. Over the past decade, the field has evolved from task-specific models toward increasingly unified and generalizable correspondence models, with recent progress further driven by the emergence of vision foundation models (VFMs). Despite these advances, existing studies remain highly diverse in their problem formulations, model architectures, training paradigms, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to obtain a unified understanding of the field. In this survey, we present a unified review of cross-view feature matching. We first introduce a structured taxonomy covering feature extraction, single-type feature matcher, multi-type feature matcher, VFMs based methods, training strategy and robust estimation, providing a coherent framework for analysis and comparison. We further examine recent advances, distilling key design principles and highlighting the shift toward unified and generalizable correspondence models. We also provide a unified experimental benchmarking of representative state-of-the-art methods under consistent protocols, enabling fair and comprehensive performance comparisons. In addition, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including efficiency, robustness under extreme conditions, and cross-domain generalization. This survey aims to provide a comprehensive and structured reference for understanding the evolution, current landscape, and future development of cross-view feature matching in the era of vision foundation models.

</details>


### [275] [Two-stage Odd Residual Flows for Mean-Preserving Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11114)
*Kiran Madhusudhanan,Christian Klötergens,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TORF框架，将平均预测与不确定性估计解耦：第一阶段用预训练确定性模型产生精确均值，第二阶段用严格奇函数的受限归一化流学习残差分布，在无需采样的前提下保证均值保持，同时实现高精度点预测和强密度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测方法在分布灵活性和均值预测精度之间存在根本性权衡：传统参数方法（如MVE）在联合NLL训练下点精度下降，而现代生成模型（如Normalizing Flows、Diffusion Models）依赖昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样且均值估计次优。

Method: 两阶段残差归一化流（TORF）：第一阶段使用预训练的确定性模型生成准确均值预测；第二阶段使用具有严格奇函数的受限归一化流学习围绕点预测的灵活残差分布，通过奇函数结构保证均值保持，无需采样即可推断。

Result: 实验结果表明TORF在短程和长程预测中均达到最先进的确定性精度（NMAE），同时提供强密度估计性能（CRPS）。

Conclusion: TORF成功实现了均值预测与不确定性估计的解耦，在不牺牲点预测精度的前提下获得了灵活的残差分布建模能力，为风险敏感的长期预测任务提供了一种高效且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting plays an essential role in risk-sensitive decision-making, particularly in long-horizon settings. However, existing approaches often face a fundamental trade-off between distributional flexibility and accurate mean prediction. Traditional parametric methods, such as Mean Variance Estimation (MVE), can suffer from degraded point accuracy when trained under joint Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) objectives, while modern-flexible generative models, including Normalizing Flows and Diffusion Models, typically rely on costly Monte Carlo sampling and may yield suboptimal mean estimates. To address this limitation, we propose Two-stage Odd Residual Flows (TORF), a framework that decouples mean forecasting from uncertainty estimation. In the first stage, a pre-trained deterministic model is used to produce an accurate mean prediction. In the second stage, a Restricted Normalizing Flow, with strictly odd functions learns flexible residual distributions around the point forecast, guaranteeing mean preservation from the first stage without sampling. Experiments show that TORF achieves state-of-the-art deterministic accuracy (NMAE) while providing strong density estimation performance (CRPS) on short and long-horizon forecasting.

</details>


### [276] [A Recommendation System Approach for Interference-Robust Sensor Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11143)
*Kaan Buyukkalayci,Kyle Pak,Merve Karakas,Christina Fragouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于推荐系统思路的传感器子集选择方法，利用频带声学特征和双塔MLP架构替代RSSI测量，在户外车辆跟踪场景中相比RSSI基线提升约20%的跟踪精度，同时保持实时选择性感知所需的低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的RSSI（接收信号强度指示）低成本的声学测量方法虽可用于推荐传感器子集，但容易受到声学干扰的影响，导致推荐精度下降，需要一种更鲁棒且高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出一个受推荐系统启发的框架，利用频带声学特征替代RSSI测量，并采用双塔多层感知机（Two-Tower MLP）架构来高效地对候选传感器子集进行评分。

Result: 在户外车辆跟踪部署实验中，所提方法相比RSSI基线将跟踪精度提升了约20%，同时保持了实时选择性感知所需的低计算开销。

Conclusion: 频带声学特征结合双塔MLP架构是一种有效的传感器子集选择方案，既能克服RSSI方法对声学干扰敏感的问题，又能保持实时性，为传感器选择性感知提供了一种可行的新思路。

Abstract: This paper develops a method for sensor-subset selection for tracking. Prior work showed that low-cost acoustic Received Signal Strength Indicator (RSSI) measurements can be used to recommend subsets of sensor nodes whose expensive sensing modalities, such as cameras, can achieve high tracking accuracy. While efficient, RSSI-based approaches are challenged by acoustic interference. We propose a recommendation-system-inspired framework that instead leverages frequency-band acoustic features and a Two-Tower Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) architecture to efficiently score candidate sensor subsets. Experimental results on outdoor vehicle-tracking deployments show that the proposed method can improve accuracy by around 20\% over the RSSI baseline while maintaining the low computational overhead required for real-time selective sensing.

</details>


### [277] [DACRI: Decision-Aware Causal Intervention Ranking for Critical Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11154)
*Shiqi Huang,Jiani He,Dingyan Shang,Yihua Xu,Jize Li,Yan Lyu,Lashimi Muraleedharan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CriticalSCM-Bench v1，一个带因果真值的可控合成供应链基准，用于评估干预选择策略。结果表明LambdaMART在半导体和关键材料场景中优于全信息静态基准（中位归一化净值提升5.7-16.2%），但在数字基础设施场景中，领域先验的恒定缓冲策略仍更优；模型在部分/延迟信息下保留33-75%的完整信息价值，关键材料场景的外分布泛化最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有供应链韧性研究大多聚焦于检测或归因供应链中断，但检测到中断并不等同于能选出使可恢复净值最大化的干预措施。论文旨在填补从"识别问题"到"选择最优干预"之间的鸿沟，并系统评估自适应排序模型相对简单结构策略的增量价值。

Method: 构建具有因果真值（causal ground truth）的可控合成基准，配备成对的事实/反事实轨迹（paired factual/counterfactual rollouts）和显式净价值目标。采用LambdaMART排序模型作为自适应策略，与全信息训练选择的静态基准以及领域先验的恒定缓冲策略进行对比，并通过部分/延迟信息设置、压力测试（干预保真度、时机、成本、未见过中断）以及受保护的生成解释研究（540次生成）进行系统评估。

Result: 在与全信息静态基准的对比中，LambdaMART在中位归一化净值上提升5.7-16.2%，其中半导体和关键材料场景具有成对统计支持，但数字基础设施场景不显著；后者中恒定缓冲策略仍更强。部分/延迟设置下LambdaMART保留33-75%的完整信息价值。压力测试显示干预保真度、时机、成本及外分布中断可改变策略排序，关键材料场景的外分布保留最弱。540次生成实验经确定性验证和模板回退后保全了所有固定干预决策，但精确措辞不稳定。

Conclusion: 在受控设置下，研究识别出自适应排序（如LambdaMART）能带来增量价值的场景（半导体、关键材料），以及简化结构策略（如恒定缓冲）仍更优的场景（数字基础设施），表明模型复杂度并非总能获得一致优势。该基准为供应链干预选择提供了可复现的评估框架，并强调需要根据领域特征权衡模型复杂性与简单稳健策略。

Abstract: Detecting or attributing a supply-chain disruption is not the same as selecting the intervention that maximizes recoverable net value. We present CriticalSCM-Bench v1, a controlled synthetic benchmark with causal ground truth, paired factual/counterfactual rollouts, and an explicit net-value objective. Relative to a full-information train-selected static benchmark, LambdaMART improves median normalized net value by 5.7--16.2\%, with paired statistical support on the semiconductor and critical-material archetypes but not on digital infrastructure. On digital infrastructure, a domain-informed constant-buffer policy remains stronger, showing that greater model complexity is not uniformly justified. Across partial and delayed settings, LambdaMART retains 33--75\% of full-clamp value. Stress tests further show that intervention fidelity, timing, cost, and held-out disruptions can alter policy ordering. Critical materials show the weakest out-of-distribution retention. Separately, a guarded explanation study over 540 generations preserves every fixed intervention decision after deterministic validation and template fallback, although exact wording remains unstable. Within this controlled setting, the results identify regimes in which adaptive ranking adds value and those in which simpler structural policies remain preferable.

</details>


### [278] [Hierarchical Empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes: Minimax Smoothing and Calibration with AODE Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11162)
*Nguyen Thai Anh,Truong Viet Vu,Tran Thien Thanh,Vo Nguyen Quoc Bao,Ngo Hoang Tu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层次经验贝叶斯朴素贝叶斯（HEB-NB）分类器，通过Type-II极大似然自适应学习Dirichlet先验浓度，替代传统固定的平滑强度，解决了高基数表格数据上平滑参数固定导致非渐近偏差的问题。理论证明其ℓ1误差界匹配经验分布极小极大速率，且在有限样本下与Laplace平滑存在严格分离；在31个基准数据集上，HEB-NB在对数损失和校准误差方面显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朴素贝叶斯分类器使用的平滑规则（如Laplace、Lidstone、Krichevsky-Trofimov和m估计）均采用固定的平滑强度，忽略了特征基数、样本量和类别不平衡等因素，在高基数现代表格数据上产生不可消失的偏差。这一问题限制了NB分类器在复杂分类任务中的概率准确性和校准性能。

Method: 提出HEB-NB：每个类别-特征条件概率用Dirichlet先验平滑，其浓度参数通过Type-II极大似然数据自适应学习，实现跨类别的信息共享，同时保持闭式推断。进一步扩展为HEB-AODE（平均一依赖估计器），将自适应平滑迁移到NB的结构松弛模型中。理论分析包括非渐近ℓ1误差界、Laplace紧下界、plug-in超额贝叶斯风险界（通过总变差张量化）以及总体top-1期望校准误差推论。实践上结合互信息加权以降低校准误差。

Result: 在31个UCI和OpenML基准数据集上，HEB-NB在概率性指标上取得最佳平均Friedman排名，在高基数数据集上对数损失降低最高22.1%，HEB-AODE相对普通AODE一致提升。结合互信息加权后，top-1期望校准误差减少41%-70%。理论上，HEB-NB的误差界匹配经验分布极小极大速率，并在有限样本风险层面与Laplace存在严格分离。

Conclusion: 数据自适应的Dirichlet平滑方案能够显著超越固定强度的传统平滑方法，在概率准确性和校准两方面都取得实质性提升。该框架不仅适用于标准NB，还能扩展到结构松弛模型（如AODE），为高基数表格数据上的概率分类提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: The Naive Bayes (NB) classifier remains a standard choice for categorical data, yet its widely used smoothing rules, such as Laplace, Lidstone, Krichevsky-Trofimov, and the $m$-estimate, all prescribe a fixed smoothing strength that ignores feature cardinality, sample size, and class imbalance, inducing a non-vanishing bias on modern high-cardinality tabular data. We propose hierarchical empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes (HEB-NB), in which each class-feature conditional probability is smoothed by a Dirichlet prior whose concentration is learned data-adaptively via Type-II maximum likelihood, enabling principled information sharing across classes while retaining closed-form inference. We further introduce HEB average one-dependence estimators (HEB-AODE), showing that the adaptive smoothing transfers cleanly to structural relaxations of NB. Theoretically, we establish a non-asymptotic $\ell_1$ error bound for HEB-NB matching the empirical-distribution minimax rate plus a vanishing data-adaptive bias, together with a matching Laplace-tight lower bound that yields a finite-sample, risk-level strict separation from Laplace. We further derive a plug-in excess Bayes-risk bound via total-variation tensorization and a population top-1 expected calibration error (ECE) corollary. Empirically, across 31 UCI and OpenML benchmarks, HEB-NB attains the best average Friedman rank on probabilistic metrics, with up to 22.1% log-loss reductions on high-cardinality datasets and consistent improvements of HEB-AODE over vanilla AODE. Combining HEB-NB with mutual-information weighting reduces top-1 ECE by 41%-70%, demonstrating substantial gains in probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


### [279] [Beyond a Bag of Features: Set-Level Instability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11197)
*Nikolai Bolik,Lennart Stöpler,Artur Andrzejak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Shani等人(2026)关于LLM表示能否恢复人类概念结构的研究，将原本基于稠密表示的余弦相似度方法替换为基于稀疏自编码器(SAE)活跃隐变量集合的重叠度作为更可解释的相似性度量。研究发现，尽管SAE隐变量集合在受控玩具模型和自然文本中能恢复组成性结构，但在人类概念分析中，SAE激活集合并不能比稠密嵌入或残差流状态更忠实地恢复人类类别边界或内部典型性结构，反而追踪的是模型内部的相似性结构。进一步的受控语义修改实验揭示了人类概念变化判断与SAE活跃集合变化之间存在显著不匹配，表明在理想化环境之外，SAE特征并不通过简单的词袋式特征语义进行组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shani等人(2026)发现LLM表示能大致恢复人类类别边界但无法反映细粒度的典型性结构，但其分析基于稠密表示的余弦相似度，缺乏可解释性。本文旨在用SAE活跃隐变量集合的重叠度作为更可解释的相似性度量，重新审视这一结论，并探究SAE特征是否真的能按照人类可理解的方式组合并反映人类概念结构。

Method: 首先在受控玩具模型中验证SAE隐变量集合级度量能否恢复类似并集(union-like)的组成性结构，并在自然文本中检验其能否产生语义连贯的邻域。然后，将人类概念分析扩展到SAE集合相似性上，系统比较SAE激活集合与稠密嵌入、残差流状态在恢复人类类别边界和类别内典型性方面的表现。最后，在受控语义修改条件下研究活跃隐变量集合的变化，并将其与人类对概念变化的判断进行对比。

Result: SAE隐变量集合能够恢复玩具模型中的组成性结构并在自然文本中诱导语义连贯的邻域。然而，在人概念分析中，SAE激活集合并不能比稠密嵌入或残差流状态更忠实地恢复人类类别边界或类别内典型性，而是追踪模型内部的相似性结构。受控语义修改实验表明，人类对概念变化的判断与SAE活跃集合的变化之间存在显著不匹配。

Conclusion: 在理想化设定之外，SAE特征并不通过简单的词袋式(bag-of-features)语义进行组合。尽管SAE在可解释性和组成性上具有潜力（在受控条件下可恢复并集结构），但其活跃集合在反映人类概念结构方面并不优于稠密嵌入，表明需要更复杂的SAE特征组合模型来弥合机器表示与人类概念认知之间的差距。

Abstract: Shani et al. (2026) show that LLM representations broadly recover human category boundaries, while failing to reflect fine-grained typicality structure. Their analysis uses cosine similarity over dense model representations. We revisit their approach using overlap over active sparse autoencoder (SAE) latent sets as a more interpretable similarity measure. We first verify that this set-level measure is meaningful: SAE latent sets can recover union-like compositional structure in controlled toy models and induce semantically coherent neighborhoods in natural text. Extending the human-concepts analysis to SAE set similarities, we find that SAE activation sets do not recover human category boundaries or within-category typicality more faithfully than dense embeddings or residual-stream states, but instead track model-internal similarity structure. To probe this gap further, we study active latent sets under well-controlled semantic modifications, revealing a substantial mismatch between human judgements of conceptual change and change in the SAE active set. We interpret this as evidence that, outside idealised settings, SAE features do not compose via simple bag-of-features semantics.

</details>


### [280] [The Gaussian-Multinoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine: A Potts Model Extension of the GRBM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11635)
*Nikhil Kapasi,Mohamed Elfouly,William Whitehead,Luke Theogarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯-多努利受限玻尔兹曼机（GM-RBM），将GB-RBM中的二元隐藏单元替换为q状态分类（Potts）单元，以支持更丰富的多值离散潜在表示，并在联想回忆和结构化记忆基准上达到与GB-RBM相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现实任务（如联想记忆、符号推理）受益于离散的结构化表示，但标准连续潜在模型难以表达此类表示；即使使用离散隐单元，二元（binary）隐单元的表达能力也有限，难以捕捉多值概念，因此需要引入q状态分类隐单元的生成式能量模型。

Method: 提出GM-RBM，扩展GB-RBM将隐藏单元替换为q状态Potts单元；给出能量函数、条件分布和参数学习规则的完整推导；采用对比散度（CD）配合温度退火和槽内多样性约束以避免状态坍缩；在容量匹配和参数匹配两种设置下与同等潜在分配数的GB-RBM进行对比评估。

Result: 在类比回忆和结构化记忆基准上，GM-RBM在相同容量下达到与GB-RBM竞争性的性能，并在多个场景下获得更优的召回率，同时训练成本相当且仅使用Gibbs更新；离散q元形式还便于高效实现。

Conclusion: 分类隐藏单元为可处理RBM中的离散推断提供了简单、可扩展且优于二元潜在单元的替代方案，研究结果阐明了在何种条件下分类隐单元更具优势。

Abstract: Many real-world tasks, from associative memory to symbolic reasoning, benefit from discrete, structured representations that standard continuous latent models can struggle to express. We introduce the Gaussian-Multinoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine (GM-RBM), a generative energy-based model that extends the Gaussian-Bernoulli RBM (GB-RBM) by replacing binary hidden units with q-state categorical (Potts) units, yielding a richer latent state space for multivalued concepts. We provide a self-contained derivation of the energy, conditional distributions, and learning rules, and detail practical training choices (contrastive divergence with temperature annealing and intra-slot diversity constraints) that avoid state collapse. To separate architectural effects from sheer latent capacity, we evaluate under both capacity-matched and parameter-matched setups, comparing GM-RBM with GB-RBM configured to have the same number of possible latent assignments. On analogical recall and structured memory benchmarks, GM-RBM achieves competitive, and in several regimes improved, recall at equal capacity with comparable training cost, despite using only Gibbs updates. The discrete q-ary formulation is also amenable to efficient implementation. These results clarify when categorical hidden units provide a simple, scalable alternative to binary latents for discrete inference within tractable RBMs.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Deciding When to Switch: E-Processes for Adaptive Minimax Training for Generative Adversarial Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10096)
*Hyunjoo Kim,Sicheng Wu,Agastya Venkatraman,Guang Lin,Sehwan Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于e-process的自适应GAN训练框架，将判别器与生成器的更新切换问题转化为序贯假设检验，在训练过程中动态决定何时切换，提供随时有效的I类错误控制，并在多模态合成分布和图像基准数据集上优于或匹配最佳固定比例基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据科学中许多假设检验问题无法在预定统计模型中以参数形式自然表述，如优化算法的动态评估。GAN训练需要在判别器和生成器之间动态切换更新，但现有方法通常依赖固定更新比例或启发式准则，缺乏统计保证。

Method: 将GAN训练中的切换问题形式化为序贯假设检验：判别器更新时，用一个e-process检验"判别器引起的经验数据分布与生成器分布之间的分离度低于目标水平"的零假设；生成器更新时（判别器固定），用第二个e-process检验反向零假设"分离度高于刷新水平"。利用条件e-value在自适应模型更新和数据依赖切换下累积为e-process，实现anytime-valid的I类错误控制。

Result: 在多模态合成分布和图像基准数据集上，该方法在多种广泛使用的GAN目标函数下匹配或优于最佳固定比例基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的e-process自适应训练方法为GAN的判别器-生成器切换提供了有统计保证的框架，无需依赖固定比例或启发式准则，在保持或提升性能的同时提供随时有效的错误控制。

Abstract: Modern data science increasingly gives rise to hypothesis-testing problems that are not naturally formulated in terms of parameters within prespecified statistical models. One important example is the dynamic evaluation of optimization algorithms, where decisions must be made during training about whether further updates remain beneficial or the algorithm should switch to a different phase. This issue is particularly relevant in stochastic min-max optimization. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) provide a canonical example, as their training requires repeated decisions about when to switch between discriminator and generator updates, yet existing methods typically rely on fixed update ratios or heuristic criteria. We formulate this switching problem as sequential hypothesis testing and develop an e-process-based adaptive training procedure. During discriminator updates, one e-process tests the null that the discriminator-induced separation between the empirical data distribution and the generator law remains below a target level. During generator updates, with the discriminator fixed, a second e-process tests the reverse null that this separation remains above a refresh level. Conditional on the observed training sample, we prove that fresh empirical indices and latent draws yield conditional e-values that can be accumulated into e-processes, providing anytime-valid Type I error control under adaptive model updates and data-dependent switching. Across multimodal synthetic distributions and image benchmark datasets, the proposed method matches or outperforms the best fixed-ratio baselines under several widely used GAN objectives.

</details>


### [282] [Iterative Erasure Count Is Not an Affine-Invariant Concept Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10566)
*Tingan Jin,Shuhang Dong,Haosong Li,Chung-Hsien Chou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了"概念维度"这一表述具有误导性：逐步擦除探针方向所得到的停止计数和累积删除秩在信息保持的可逆重参数化下会发生变化，因此这些量是过程相关（procedure-relative）的估计量，而非概念本身的内在语义维度。作者区分了模型定义量（生成维度、充分线性维度、最小守卫秩）与过程定义量（停止计数、累积编辑秩），并通过高斯构造、全QR过程及V-JEPA2实验加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经表示在编码一个概念时使用了多少个方向？以往研究通过反复擦除探针方向并报告停止计数或累积删除秩来回答，但作者怀疑这些量并非概念内在属性，而是受重参数化影响的任意量。该问题关系到我们对神经网络可解释性与语义表示理解的可靠性。

Method: 作者采用理论分析与受控实验相结合的方法：(1) 区分模型定义量（生成维度、充分线性维度、最小守卫秩）与过程定义量（停止计数、累积编辑秩）；(2) 在群体高斯构造中，利用可逆剪切变换保持预测问题不变，但改变累积欧氏擦除计数；(3) 针对双输出全QR过程展示累积编辑秩从2变为环境维度4；(4) 证明完整累积度量QR轨迹在度量、探针、正则化器和打破平局规则一致迁移时是仿射等变的；(5) 在已知秩有限样本Adam/QR校准中测试不同剪切参数下的更新次数；(6) 对冻结的V-JEPA2特征进行受控重参数化，检验实际优化下的轨迹变化。

Result: (1) 累积欧氏擦除计数在可逆剪切下从1变为2，但预测问题和三个模型定义量保持不变；(2) 分离现象在Moore-Penrose OLS和所有有限非负脊权重下成立；(3) 双输出全QR过程中累积编辑秩从2变为环境维度4；(4) 协方差并非规范的语义度量，只是仿射等变性的一个推论；(5) 已知秩校准中，恒等混合在全部20次大样本运行中停止于1次更新，而所有测试剪切均接受至少2次更新；(6) 对V-JEPA2特征的重参数化在保持秩零预测的同时改变了欧氏轨迹。

Conclusion: 迭代擦除方法返回的是一个由表示几何与完整测量过程共同决定的过程相对估计量，本身不能作为语义维度的可靠度量。研究提醒我们，在解释神经表示时应区分模型的内在结构属性与测量过程引入的任意性。

Abstract: How many directions does a neural representation use to encode a concept? A common answer repeatedly erases probe directions and reports the stopping count or cumulative removed rank. We show that both quantities can change under an information-preserving invertible reparameterization, so neither is intrinsically a concept dimension. We distinguish model-defined population quantities (generating dimension, sufficient linear dimension, and minimum guarding rank) from procedure-defined quantities such as stopping count and cumulative edit rank. In a population Gaussian construction, an invertible shear preserves the prediction problem and all three quantities, yet changes the cumulative Euclidean erasure count from one to two. The separation holds for Moore--Penrose ordinary least squares and every finite nonnegative ridge weight. For a two-output full-QR procedure matching our motivating video analysis, cumulative edit rank similarly changes from two to the ambient dimension four. Conversely, the complete cumulative metric-QR trajectory is affine-equivariant when its positive-definite metric, probe, regularizer, and tie-breaking are transported consistently; exact covariance is one corollary, not a canonical semantic metric. In a known-rank finite-sample Adam/QR calibration, identity mixing stops after one accepted update in all 20 large-sample runs, whereas each tested shear $a\in\{.5,.75,1,1.25,2\}$ accepts at least two updates in all 20 runs. Controlled reparameterizations of frozen V-JEPA2 features preserve rank-zero predictions yet alter later Euclidean trajectories under practical optimization. These visual contact experiments are stress tests, not estimates of contact dimension. Iterative erasure therefore returns a procedure-relative estimand jointly determined by representation geometry and the full measurement procedure, not a semantic dimension by itself.

</details>


### [283] [Spectral Embeddings of Degree-$α$ Laplacians in Random Dot Product Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10845)
*John Park,Ning Hao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了网络数据的谱嵌入中一系列度归一化方法的统一定理。在随机点积图模型下，建立了该族嵌入的行向中心极限定理，并揭示了度归一化如何同时影响总体几何和节点的局部不确定性。通过双社区随机块模型下的投影高斯贝叶斯误差诊断，证明了不存在普遍最优的归一化方法，其优劣取决于网络密度、社区不平衡度和块概率结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱聚类方法通常依赖少数几种矩阵表示（如邻接矩阵和对称拉普拉斯矩阵），但不同度归一化方法对聚类性能的影响缺乏统一的分布论理解。作者希望建立一个涵盖这些常见方法的连续谱系框架，从理论上解释何时以及为何某种归一化方法更优。

Method: (1) 构建一个包含邻接矩阵嵌入和对称拉普拉斯嵌入等作为特例的度归一化谱嵌入连续族；(2) 在随机点积图（RDPG）模型下推导该嵌入族的行向中心极限定理；(3) 利用极限分布，在双社区随机块模型（SBM）中通过投影高斯贝叶斯误差诊断比较不同归一化方法的性能。

Result: 研究发现不存在单一归一化方法在所有情形下都表现最优。归一化方法的优劣取决于网络密度、社区不平衡度和块概率结构——通常在低密度或社区更不平衡的网络中，更强的度归一化（更接近拉普拉斯归一化）表现更优。中心极限定理提供了对归一化影响的显式描述。

Conclusion: 该工作为度归一化谱嵌入提供了统一的分布论理解框架，从理论上阐明了替代归一化方法在谱聚类中何时以及为何能够改进性能，为实践中根据网络特性选择归一化策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Spectral clustering methods for network data are commonly based on a few matrix representations, such as the adjacency matrix and the symmetric Laplacian. We study a continuum of degree-normalized spectral embeddings that includes these commonly used choices as special cases. Under a random dot product graph model, we establish a row-wise central limit theorem for this family of embeddings. The result provides an explicit description of how degree normalization affects both population geometry and the local uncertainty of embedded nodes. We use the limiting distributions to compare different normalizations in two-community stochastic block models through a projected-Gaussian Bayes-error diagnostic. These comparisons show that no single normalization is uniformly preferred. Instead, the favored normalization depends on network density, community imbalance, and block-probability structure. Typically, stronger normalization is favored in lower-density or more imbalanced settings. These results provide a unified distributional understanding of when and why alternative normalizations may improve spectral clustering.

</details>


### [284] [Self-Normalized Inference for Constant-Stepsize Temporal-Difference Learning under Markovian Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10896)
*Min Zeng,Yichen Zhang,Xiaofeng Shao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了常步长时序差分（TD）学习在单条马尔可夫轨迹上的统计推断问题，建立了泛函中心极限定理，并提出了基于Richardson-Romberg（RR）加速的联合泛函极限与布朗桥自标准化方法，实现了无需估计长程协方差或选择带宽/批长度的渐近枢轴置信区域，同时支持单遍实现且内存不随轨迹长度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常步长TD学习在实际中广泛应用，但从单条马尔可夫轨迹进行统计推断时面临两大挑战：一是序列相关性使得传统i.i.d.假设失效；二是步长依赖的平稳目标使得估计量收敛到与原贝尔曼解不同的目标。现有推断方法通常需要估计长程协方差或选择带宽/批长度，这些过程繁琐且对有限样本表现敏感。

Method: 首先对固定步长线性TD建立泛函中心极限定理，其协方差保留由随机TD矩阵和稳态迭代误差引起的乘性分量。随后推导由同一条轨迹驱动的并行Richardson-Romberg递归的联合泛函极限。利用布朗桥自标准化构造渐近枢轴的置信区域，无需估计长程协方差或选择带宽/批长度。对于给定的状态值对比，实现单遍算法且内存不随轨迹长度增长。此外研究水平索引设计，其中步长在每次运行内保持不变并随更长水平递减，在显式RR依赖的速率窗口下，残余RR目标偏移、乘性余项和初始化效应在根号n尺度下可忽略。

Result: 实验在FrozenLake和Garnet环境中验证了平稳目标的覆盖率、RR目标修正效果以及水平索引设计的有限样本行为。结果表明所提出的方法能够在固定步长下对RR平稳目标进行有效的置信推断，并在水平索引设计下实现对投影贝尔曼解的推理。

Conclusion: 该工作为常步长TD学习的统计推断提供了理论基础和实用工具：通过Richardson-Romberg加速与布朗桥自标准化的结合，避免了传统长程协方差估计的复杂性，实现了无需人工调参的一遍式推断框架，并给出了从常步长目标到贝尔曼解推断的理论保证。

Abstract: Constant-stepsize temporal-difference (TD) learning is attractive for policy evaluation, but inference from a single Markov trajectory must account for serial dependence and a stepsize-dependent stationary target. For fixed-stepsize linear TD, we establish a functional central limit theorem whose covariance retains the multiplicative component induced by the random TD matrix and the stationary iterate error. We then derive a joint functional limit for parallel Richardson--Romberg (RR) recursions driven by the same trajectory. A Brownian-bridge self-normalizer yields asymptotically pivotal confidence regions for prespecified state-value contrasts without estimating the long-run covariance or selecting a bandwidth or batch length. For such a contrast, the procedure admits a one-pass implementation whose memory does not grow with the trajectory length. At a fixed stepsize, the inferential center is the RR stationary target. We also study horizon-indexed designs in which the stepsize remains constant within each run and decreases across longer horizons. Under an explicit RR-dependent rate window, the residual RR target shift, multiplicative remainder, and initialization effect are negligible at the root-$n$ scale, yielding inference for the projected Bellman solution. Experiments on FrozenLake and Garnet illustrate stationary-target coverage, RR target correction, and the finite-sample behavior of the horizon-indexed design.

</details>


### [285] [Conditional Independence Tests for Constraint-Based Causal Discovery: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11156)
*Pavel Averin,Theodoros Moysiadis,Ioannis Katakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述了约束式因果发现（如PC和FCI算法）中条件独立性检验的方法论，将常用CI检验分为六大类（偏相关、列联表、回归、最近邻、核方法、机器学习），重点关注高维和混合数据类型下的假设条件、鲁棒性与可扩展性，并探讨了检验级属性（功效衰减、I/II型错误不对称）与图级错误（骨架恢复、v-结构定向）之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件独立性检验是约束式因果发现算法的统计引擎，其决策直接影响骨架剪枝和关键定向步骤。然而现有文献对CI检验的假设条件、鲁棒性局限及在高维混合数据类型（生物医学领域常见）下的表现缺乏系统性的梳理与对比，因此需要一篇聚焦于该主题的全面综述。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，将CI检验方法归类为六个家族：偏相关、列联表、回归、最近邻、核和基于机器学习的方法。对每个家族分析其假设条件、何时能正确反映数据生成分布、何时失败，并专门考察针对各家族局限的鲁棒性增强层。同时对比主要R和Python库中的采用情况。

Result: 该综述揭示了各CI检验家族在不同条件下的有效性边界，阐明了检验级属性（如功效随条件集大小衰减、I/II型错误不对称）如何传导为因果图重建中的骨架错误和v-结构定向错误，并对主流R和Python库中各类方法的采用差异进行了总结。

Conclusion: 本文指出了该领域的开放挑战，包括无需离散化的混合类型CI检验、小样本错误控制，以及提高CI检验可扩展性的策略，为该领域未来研究方向提供了清晰的路线图。

Abstract: Conditional Independence (CI) tests are the statistical engine of constraint-based causal discovery: in algorithms such as PC (Peter-Clark) and FCI (Fast Causal Inference), skeleton pruning and key orientations follow directly from CI decisions. This survey reviews CI testing with emphasis on assumptions, robustness, and scalability in high-dimensional and mixed-type settings common in biomedical domains. The survey organizes widely used CI methods into six families: partial-correlation, contingency-table, regression, nearest-neighbor, kernel, and machine-learning-based. Special emphasis is provided on the robustness layers that address the limitations of these families. For each family, the survey examines when CI decisions reflect the data-generating distribution and when they fail. By this, we link test-level properties, including power decay with conditioning set size and asymmetric type I/II error consequences, to graph-level errors in skeleton recovery and v-structure orientation. The survey also compares adoption across major R and Python libraries and summarizes open challenges, including mixed-type CI testing without discretization, small-sample error control, and strategies for improving scalability of CI-testing.

</details>
